*ALHMDULILLAH Ina godiya Ga Allah s w a da ya Bani ikon Fara rubuta wannan kirkirarren littafin ba Dan Komai ba saidan farincikinku masoya insha Allah labarin Nan salo Ne da na tabbbatar zai amshi a zukatanku kyauta ne comment dinku da zazzafan sharhi kawaii nake Bukata Banyi alkawarin posting kullum ba Amma nayi alkawarin gamashi Cikin sauri insha Allah bazan gaji da gode Muku ba bisa ga zallar Soyayyar da ba haufi a Cikinta masoyan asali ku sani miss hajo Taku CE ta har abada ban yarda wani yayi amfani da wani Bangare ko juyamun labarin muddin Hakan ya Faru Zan dauki mummunan mataki*

Babban Gida ne na Gani na fad'a da ya gaji da Kayan morewa.

Tankamemen gidane Mai dauke bangarori Da Basu wuce hud'u ba Parking space din gidan bazai Gaza Daukar mota sama da ashirin ba babban Gida ne Wanda fadar tsaruwarsa kanshi Bata Baki ne Daga Gani kasan mamallakinshi gidan ba kananun mutane bane.

Wata hamshakiyar dattijuwa ce fara sol jawur kallo Daya zakayiwa fatarta kasan ba karamun hutu take samu ba ,Yar gayu ce ta bugawa a jarida Saida da Ka kalleta kasan Bata hada ko Jini da hausawa ba Sanye take Cikin makeken lace na Alfarma danbareriyar sarkar gold Ce jikinta hannayenta Gaba D'aya Awarwarayen zinare ne Banda zobuna.

Zaune take ta hakimce a hadaddun cushion din dake shake da palourn na Alfarma Komai yaji Daga ganin furnitures din dakin kansu kasan dukiya Tayi kuka.

Pen da biro ne a Hannunta yayinda Y'ar aikinta Ke gabanta Tayi kasa da kanta Saida ta Gama rubuce rubucen kafin ta dago fuskarta da ba alamar fara'a a Cikinta ta kalli Yar Aikin ta ce"saura Kiyi mistake a abunda na rubuta Kije driver na jiranki"Cikin sauri ta Amsa Tana kakkarwa dan Tasan hajiya ba Wasa Bata rageka da komai ba ci sha sutura sadaka da sauransu Saidai Akwai Nuna Dagawa Batasan Hulda Da na kasa Da ita Dan zata iya rantsewa Bata tab'a ga fara'ar hajiyar tasu ba Tsawon shekarun ta biyu a gidan.


Kukan Jaririyar ko jaririn da ya kaurade gidan ne ya sanyata Saurin Tashi ba Shiri ta ce "Subahanallah kukan belah nake ji "har hadawa take da gudu wajen nufar Stairs.


Cikin sauri ta bude k'ofar tare da kutsa Kanta ba abunda ya Bata mamaki sai ganin yanda yarinyar ke tsala kuka a kusa dashi ya sadda Kai baiko motsa ba Cikin sauri ta Dauketa tare da sabarta a kaf'ad'a.

 da Bata wuce wata shida zuwa biyar ba jijjigata ta Fara tare da Maida dubanta ga kanshi har Lokacin Bai dago ba Cikin Wace Irin Murya ta ce

 "Aliyu ?Bai Dagoba haka Batasa ran zai Amsa mata ba "Zama Tayi ta na cigaba da jijjiga Babyn ta ce "Haba Aliyu me yasa Ka zabawa rayuwarka haka,meyasa bazaka cire mutuwar Nabila a ranka ba ,sanin kanka ne Wanda Ya mutu baya Tab'a dawowa Aliyu a Halin Yanzu Nabila ba abunda take Bukata sama Da Adduarka yawan zubda Mata hawaye da kake ba shine Soyayyar ba Addua da tawakkali shine babban abunda zaka Mata ruhinta ya kwanta Cikin salama Nabila ta Tafi Tafiyar da ba dawowa kamar yanda dukanmu Nan Jiranta muke Ka godewa Allah Da Nabila Bata tafi ba Saida ta barmana Jininta,ta bar Mana abunda Zamu dinga kallo muna Tunawa da ita".

"goge hawayen da ya zubo Mata ta gefe tayi ita Kanta tasan Daina kukan Rashin Nabila abune Mai wahala a Cikin ranta bare Shi,Amma Kuma hakuri shine maganin Komai"A Hankali ya dago tare katon frame din dake hannunshi Wanda koni Banga meye ciki ba Gaba Daya fuskarsa ta jike da hawaye Magana yake so Yayi Amma kukan da Yake cinsa Ya kasa Koda furta kalma Daya Kyakyawane ajin Karshen chocolate colour ba fari bane Haka bazaka kirashi Baki ba Fatarshi kawai zaka kalla kasan ba karamun D'an hutu da gayu bane Dukda baya Cikin nutsuwar hakan gaba Daya fuskartsa zagaye take da kyakyawan sajen da ya Kara Mata kyau da haiba Karamun lips dinshi da Yake ja kamar Yana shafa Lipstick ba abunda yafi Daukar Hankali na dashi kamar girar sa ,Irin shape din da take dashi ko mace ta samu haka ta more Aliyu Handsome guy ne kyakyawa na bugawa a jarida kallo Daya zaka Masa kasan Bai haura shekaru Talatin Da ukku ba.

Ganin yanda Yake sikewa ne ya Sanya Momy saurin aje Bela da har Lokacin kuka take fridge ta Daga ta da dauko Ruwan sanyi a bottle ta zuba a glass cup din dake saman fridge din Tana nufarsa tare da sa Masa cup din a Baki a Hankali Take Daddabar bayansa Cikin sigar lallashi ta ce.

 "calm down habiby,calm down sorry sorry"kawai take fad'a Ya na Shan Ruwan yaji zuciyarshi Tayi sanyi ganin yanda Yake sauke Ajiyar Zuciya ne Saida ya Gama Shan Ruwan Kafin ya dago Saurin goge Masa hawayen Tayi ta na sakar Masa Murmushi ta ce


 "bansan Jarumi na Da rauni ba Ina son kullum zuciyar habiby na Ta Kasance jajurtacciya Ka kwanta kayi bacci Nasan Jiya baka samu bacci ba Oya Yaron momy"ta fada ta na sakinsa "Kamar yanda ta fada haka Yayi ya kwanta kamar wani yaro Kafin ta ja Masa blanket ta na rage gudun AC ta yo Saurin Daukar Kyakyawar farar yarinya Mai zubi sak da Yaran Larabawa ta ce

 "Sorry Belah na Muje naji waya tabamun ke"Suna fitowa kasa Taci Karo da Yar aikinta Ta na Shigowa Da Kayan da ta aiketa Cikin hanzari ta ce "Jamila Yi sauri had'awa Bela  Abincinta aidai kin sayo Komai "Eh Hajiya ta fada ta na nufar Aikin da ta sanyata 

Cikin kankanin lokaci ta Gama ta na Mika Mata da kanta ta dinga feeding yarinyar har ta Gama Bata Wanda Da Kanta ta ture Kafin ta Bata peck a kumatu ta ce "good girl babyna Allah Miki albarka"zillo yarinyar ta Fara ta na dariyar jikinta ta na son Janyo Mata Dan kunnenta

 "Jamila zoki amshi yarinyar Nan na shirya wajen Aiki Kar na makara"Tana mikata Taki zuwa ta sa kuka "OMG ta fada ta ce "jeki kawai mtswww na Rasa ubanme kikewa yarinyar nan da take gudunki Tak'i yarda Da nanny Kwata-kwata yarinya"Kafin ta harari yarinyar ta ce .

"kin Hana mommy zuwa wajen Aiki ko Keda dadynki"lafewa Tayi jikinta ta na luma babbar Yatsarta a hannu Batayi kokarin hanata ba Dan Bazata hanu ba tun ta na jinjira ta Saba Da tsotsar yatsa Kayan wasanta tasa aka dauko ta Baza Mata Kafin kace me Ta hau wasanta Hankali kwance ta na zullo daka kalli yarinyar kasan Akwai isashiyar lafiya tattare da ita.

Dattijuwa Mata ce bak'a Irin black beauty dinnan  da Bata haura shekaru arba'in ko k'asa da haka ba a duniya zaune take gaban Tap ga uban wanki tuli-tuli a gabanta Dirja kawai take Dan tun safe Take Aikin nan gashi har azahar Ta wuce Bata samu ta Gama ba

 Jin saukar mutum a Bayanta ana shashekar ne ya sanyata tsagaita wankin tare da tsame hannunta Ta juyo gareta tare da son ta dagota ta kasa Cikin Murya Mai rauni ta ce "Nasreen me ya Faru eh y'an Mata na "Cikin shsheka da Muryar kuka ta ce.

 "mama me yasa Mutane bazasu Daina Kirana Da shegiya mayya ba,mama me yasa bazasu Gane Ni kalar Tawa kaddarar ba Mama Ni Nace a haife Ni ba uba,ko Ni na zabawa kaina Hakan ,mama me yasa bazasu Daina Kirana da Hakan ba bazan Kara Komawa Makarantar ba Tunda Nan d'inma da muka dawo bamu tsira ba na Riga na hakura da rayuwar y"ancin da kike so inyi mama Saboda bani da Shi na hakura".

"Ta karasa fada Cikin kuka Zazzakar muryarta da ita Tafi tsayamun a Rai,Daman da gaske Akwai masu Murya da ake Kira kamar Busar sarewa?na tambayi kaina.
Fara'ar ar matar ne Gaba d'aya ta dauke Cikin kasalalliyar Murya ta ce "Nasren Ki dago ki kalle Ni"Ba Shiri ta dago fuskarta Jab'e Jab'e da hawaye
" tsarki Ya tabbata ga ubangiji Sammai da kassai Mahaliccin wannan zankadediyar surar A Gaskiya Zan iya cewa Tsawon rayuwata da kallace kallacena bantaba Gani ko Jin labarin Akwai kyau Irin na yarinyar Nan ba Kyakyawa ce ta gagara misali na tabbata ko da Ita Za ace ta yo Kanta da kanta bata Isa ta bawa kyanta haka ba Dan Gaba Daya kyan ya wuce misali Da mizani ,Kyakyawace ajin Karshe Fara sol ba alamun ko tabo a fuskarta dara-daran magananta masu Dauke da zara-zaran gashin Ido Karamun bakinta  ga dogon hancinta siriri round face Dinta Motsa bakinta Tayi Wanda Hakan ya Bawa dimples Dinta Daman lotsawa gabaki Daya kaga yarinyar Ko giyar wake kasha baka Isa Ka Sata a sahun jerin Hausawa ba sak balarabiya zaka kirata.

 "na am mama"ta fada Cikin rawar Murya "Rasa Abun cewa Mama Tayi ji take Ita Kanta kamar Tayi kukan ,da Bawa Yana da ikon chanzawa Kansa kaddara da ta Jima da chanza Tata da ta y'arta tana Kama hannunta "muje nasan Akwai Yunwa tattare dake"shine kawai abunda ta iya fad'a ta na Jan hannunta Suka nufi wani karamun daki dake chan Bayan Babban gidan Saida ta Mike Naga Tsawonta da Kuma diri Masha Allah kawai shine abunda na iya fad'a.
Samira Irin ta, da ta Janyo tare da budewa bayan sun zauna lafiyayyar shinkafa da Miya ce sai kamshi ke Tashi ta bayyana Tuni Miyan Nasren Ya biya Daman Yunwa take ji janyo kwanan Tayi ta na Saurin Sa hannu

Ita ta tayata suka Gama wankin tas dukda ta na cewa ta huta ta kwaso gajiya ta ce "a a mama ai bangaji ba "dukda Akwai gajiyar tattare da ita.

Lokacin da Yaran gidan KE tafiya islamiya Daidai Lokacin da Sukayi Zaune a d'aki mama nayi Mata Karin karatu kamar yanda take Mata kullum suka Gama ta Bata Haddar da ta biya Mata kafin ta Mike Ta ce"Bari naje na D'aura sanwar dare "Mikewa tayi Gabanta na Faduwa Dan ta tsani mutanen gidan Ba kamar yanda suka tsaneta dukda a wajensu suka samu inuwar da Basu Taba Samun Irinta ba rayuwarsu ta baya ta ce.

 "Muje mama na tayaki"Bata Musa Mata ba Sukayi cikin gidan.

Gaban tane ya Fad'i ganin ashraf a palourn Tare da Zuly da tunda ta shigo take aika Mata Harara a rayuwar Zuly ta tsani Nasreen Saboda kyawunta A ganinta samarinsu na ganin wannan me zasuyi da ita dukda Basu Da Muni babban makasudin Karin tsanarma Na Lokacin da saurayinta Yazo zance Lokacin ta fito an aiketa ya dinga yabo da santin NASREN kullum yazo saiya Mata zancenta Wanda Daga karshe tace Masa Baki ne Kuma sun Tafi tun daga ranar Bai Kara zuwa ba da ta dameshi a waya ma cewa yayi Shi ya daina sonta Daman Akwai latsa-latsan manyan Yara a duniya bai sani ba Shi ya hakura da ita zai Nemo Balarabiyar duk inda take.


Dogon tsaki taja ganin yanda Ashraf har wani hadiya yau yake ganinta yasa Zuly ta banka Mata Harara tayi Ciki .

Yana Sosa Kai ya ce "Mama Ina yini"Murmushi Tayi ta ce "lafiya qlau Ashraf Ya Aiki "ta na yin Cikin kitchen din bata jira Amsar sa ba Saurin bin hanyar mama tayi wanda taku baifi biyu ya cimmata ba tare da ware hannayensa biyu Yana sakar Mata wani killer Murmushi ya ce ".
Ina kuma zaki My feture wife Bayan kin ganni Sarai "Tana hade fuska ta ce "Aiki Zan Taya mama please ka bani hanya"Yana kamo hannunta ya ce"na fad'a maki kalarki ba ta shiga Kitchen bace ,mama ma da Munyi Aure ta Daina duk wani Aikin da take tunda ta Zama surukar Ashraf sani D'an chanji".

Cikin sauri ta fusge hannunta Cikin fushi ta ce "na fada maka karka Kara tabani Ashraf ko ni maharramarka CE ha'a"ta fad'a ta na tsatsareshi da Dara daran Idanunta Saurin hade hannayensa yayi biyu wajen bata hakuri ya ce.

Pa "sorry my wife laifin Zuciyata NE kinsan Bata da Kashi a kanki"Tana yamutsa fuska ta ce "Ni ba matar ka bace"Yana sakin Murmushi ya ce "Ai kinkusa Zama inshallah duk laifin daddy ne da ya kasa fahimtar kalar son da nake Maki yake jamun Rai Amma bank'i a Yanzu an D'aura mana Aure ba....."Aure da uwarwa za a Daura maka salamamme Sakaran banza Da ka rako maza duniya to inma kana mafarki Ka na hasashe tun wuri Ka Daina Dan Karya kake Ka b'ata mun zuri'a da mugun iri,kamar yanda yake haramun Auren wacce kuke Ciki d'aya haka aurenka da wannan shegiyar balbelar marar asali na haramta maka".

"suka tsinkayi Muryar Dattijuwar dake magana ta na nunasu Cikin tsananin fushi kafin ta buga Mata tsawa ta ce "wuce kiban wuri farar munafuka Kurwar D'a na ahir d'inki "Har hadawa take da Gudu wajen Yin baya kamar zatayi tuntube ta bar part din yayi zai bita ta daka Masa tsawa ta ce.

"binta zakayi to bita ingani"Haba Mama wlh Abunda kike bakya kyautawa fisabillahi me tayi Miki"Uwarka tamun marar hankali fad'a zakayi in fad'a ?"Kallon Zuly dake biye da mama lami yayi Yana mata gargadi da hannu ya ce "KE Kuma sainaci Ubanki munafuka "kafin ya juya fuu yabar palourn.

Dakinsu ta fad'a Kan katifarsu Kuka ne yazo mata tayi Saurin hadiyesu kukan me zatayi,bakuwar kalmarce aka kirata dashi?yaci ace ta Saba Da Kalmar Nan Wanda ta taso Cikinta tun yarinta ake Fad'a Mata meyasa har Yanzu bazata Daina kuka da Jin ciwon an kirata da Hakan ba Saurin goge hawayenta tayi ta na Janyo jakar bokan ta Ta Fara duddubawa

Washegari a gajiye ta dawo Daga Makaranta Jikin Mamanta ta shige ta na nishi ta ce "na gaji mama"Ta na shafa Bayanta ta ce "Sannu ko yarinyar kirki ya karatun fatan kina Ganewa ko"Ta na yatsina fuska ta ce

 "Ina Ganewa mama Saidai bana Jin Dadin karatu Cikin Wanda na girma duka Gaba Daya class din nice babba Sai last week da aka kawo wata sa'a ta Shinema naji Dadi muna kawance da ita kamarni Wai s s 2 mama"Murmushi Tayi ta ce .

"Haka zakiyi hakuri ai Hamdala ya Kamata Kiyi nasren da Kika samu damar cigaba da karatun ai tsakani na da Alhaji Sani Sai fatan gamawa da duniya lafiya ya sanyaki Makaranta Daidai da yaranshi a zamaninan Ni kaina mamaki nake Daman Akwai Masu Halin kwarai Irin na Alhaji Baya Mai goya marayu Allah dai ya biyashi da Aljanna".

Murmushi tayi ta na Lefewa Jikin mahaifiyartata Tabbas ta na tuno rayuwarsu da baya da sukayita a gallafiri da Yanzu sun samu yanci dukda tsangwamar Da suke samu Daga wajen matar gidan da hajiya karama amaryar alhaji ba ruwanta Tuno da Yanda Sukayi fad'a jiya Da Sopy a Makaranta Jiya Daga uncle dinsu Ya saya Mata chocolate Ashe sonshi take ta Dauka Soyayya suke ta shaketa Cikin aji Sannan Ta fad'a Mata maganganu Ta fadawa Yan class dinsu Ita shegiyace Bata uba zaman ci Rani suke a gidansu babansune ya tallafi rayuwarta har ta samu yanci take zuwa Makaranta wanda wasu Daga Cikin class din suka hau Tafi da ihu wasu Kuma Basu yarda ba Dan Suna Ganin Karyane ace Kyan nasren ya Tafi a matsayin Nan yarinyar Nan da ko gidan president Akace ta fito baka Isa kayi gardama ba "Saurin barin tunanin wannan tayi ta Mike Dan gabatar da Salla.

Suna Zaune after Isha'i ita da mama suna bitar karatu Amer karamin Dan Hajiya karama ya shigo ya ce "Aunty Nasre Abbanmu ya dawo ya ce kizo"To kace gata Nan "cewar mama yayinda Ta kalli nasren ta ce.

 "Tashi maza Abbanku na jiranki"Mikewa Tayi Jiki a sanyaye Dan Batasan ya akayi ba har Yanzu hankalinta Bai kwanta da yangidan Nan dukda Kusan sun haura shekara Da rabi ma a gidan Har ta isa gidan ta na sake sake a ranta Gaba Daya mutanen gidan Suna palourn kanta a kasa ta gaishesu wanda Alhaji da hajiya karama ne suka samu damar Amsa mata Sauran Yan matan Na binta Da Harara Mama lami ko tsaki taja ta na Kauda Kai.

"Karaso y'ata Kunyar me kike na fad'a Miki ki Daina Kunya Nan din Gidanku ne "Jiki a sanyaye ta Karasa ta na tsugunnawa ta ce"Ina yini Abba"Ya na washe Baki da fara'a ya ce "lafiya qlau nasren Yan Mata Ya makaranta kina Maida Hankali ko"Cikin rawar Murya ta ce.

 "Eh Abba"To madalla kina bukatar wani Abu"Saurin girgiza Kai Tayi tace"a a "Nasan ko kina Bukata Bazaki fad'a ba Nasan kawaicinki Sarai amshi amshi"ya fada'ya na mika Mata sabbin dubu dubu"Girgiza Kai Tayi ta ce "a a Abba "Ke amshi bansan shiririta Kujimun yarinya Babanki yayi miki kyauta ki mayar"Tana Amsa Ta ce "Nagode Abba Allah saka da Alkhairi Ya Kara budi"yanajin Dadi har ranshi ya ce "Ameen ameen yarinyar kirki.

"Dogon tsakin Da mama lami ta ja NE ya Sanya Abba sallamar Nasren ta Tafi a hanya ta nalisssafa kudin dubu Biyar ce Ta na zuwa ta mikawa mama Tayi godiya da Addu'ar Sanya Alkhairi.

"Habiby Ya kamata Ka koma Kan aiki fa,banajin dadin ganinka haka gida Sannan compony Yana matukar bukatar ka Kasan Banason abunda zai samu targada game da componyn Nan biyu Musamman na Nan tunda chan akwai Yaya ya na lura da al'amuran dukda Bai kamata mu bar Masa Nauyi bayan Wanda Shima yake kanshi".

Numfashi ya ja ya na tsagaita wasan da yake yiwa yarshi ta na dariya ta na Kama Gemunshi tun bayan mutuwar Nabila baka Taba ganin fara'arshi inba kwanannan da yakewa little belah ba

Washegari aka kawo Yan Mata guda Biyar zaben nanny duk wacce mommy ta mikawa saita makale ta sa kuka Harda sissikewa duka Biyar din Taki yarda dasu har sikewa take Dan kukama kamar taga dodo"Cikin sauri mom ta ce.


 "Kwashesu Laure ku Tafi Yarinyar Nan Ta rikice Kar a lashemun tilon jika Kuje Nan Gaba Idan Ina Bukata na kiraki Bala driver ya Baki 20k kudin transfort please kubar Nan maza".

 "lafiya take kuka momy"Wlh kaiko Daga  nanny sunzo Ta zaba ko Da wacce zata Yarda da ita shikenan ta dinga Kuka waya sani ma ko mayu ta kwasomun su lashemun jika"maza tottofa Mata Addua kafin ta yo Saurin had,'a burner ta na sakar turaren jiji"Bari muje sama na lallabata"kawai ya fad'a ya na sama Da ita Wanda kafin suje ma tayi bacci a Kirjinta Ya kwantar Da ita Domin karta Tashi Hotunanta Gaba Daya da Wanda Sukayi tare yakebi da kallo Dake mammane Jikin Bangon dakin Baisan Lokacin da wasu hawaye masu dumi suka shiga zubo Masa ba a kasalance ya ce.


 "babu madadinki a Zuciyata my belah har yau har gobe har nabar Numfashi da soyayyarki a Raina why Mutuwa zatayi Mana yankan kauna Bayan Tasan tsananin Soyayyar dake tsakaninmu Tun kina shekara goma nake sonki bela a kanki Nasan Soyayya Kuma a kanki na Kare na tabbbatar nida samun farinciki har abada bazan Taba iya zaman Aure da ko wace mace ba Bayan bake,Gaba Daya ke naba ragamar rayuwata Tunanin Irin Rayuwar da Sukayi Bayan auransu ya dingayi.

 tuna Lokacin farkon samun Cikin Little belah yayi Ya na kwance Jikin mom ta shigo Cikin sauri da waya a hannunta gefensu ta je ta na Dora kafadarta Jikin momy ta ce "Momy see Rigar Nan zaiyiwa Unborn Dina kyau ko momy Kuma Red ce Rigar Ita zansa Mata Lokacin Birthday d'inta na cikar one year a waya na Gani Bari na Kira dad ya sayo Mana Irinta ko My hero"ta fad'a ta na Kallonshi.

"duka Dariya suka Mata Cikin zolaya ya ce "Nifa bantabaga uwar azzazabi irinki ba soulmate Cikin wata ukkun har anfaa kirawa baby shekara"Cikin shagwaba Ta kalli momy dake Murmushi ta ce "momy kin ganshi ko ,Wai karnayi Murna Kuma ai Cikin yafara fitowa ko momy ta fad'a ta na Dora Mata  hannunta a cikin.


"Rabu dashi daughter na Shi bai Waye ba har Yanzu muje muci gaba da zabin Kayan ta fad'a ta na kallon wayar"gwalo ta Masa "yayinda ya Mata nuni da Zasu had'e "Yana jinsu Suna ta zazzaban Kaya ta Cikin waya Suna gamawa ta dannawa Babanta Kira Yana Dauka Cikin sauri ta ce "Habiby Sabahul khair"Yana Amsa Mata ta ce"Ka hau what's app Abiy na tura Maka Hotunan rigunan Birthday d'in Baby Ka tayamu zabe momy ta ce sunyi kyau"Daga bangarensa ya ce

 "To masoyiya Zanhau nagani Ki kulamun da kanki Da Jikana kinji"Ta na Murna ta ce "To Abiy Harma na fad'a Masa ya ce"yana gaisheka yayi missing d'inka"Murmushin momy tayii ta na girgiza Kai Ta rasa wani Irin so NE Nabiila ta Dorawa cikin Nan dake jikinta ba Kallonta tayi ta ce "Bani Yayan mu gaisa to"Ta na Mika Mata wayar tayi Saurin Jan Hannunsa ta ce "hero muje Ka ga wani Takalmi da na sayawa baby Jiya"Binta yayi ya na Murmushi.


Gaba Daya hawayene suka wanke Masa fuska Bayan dawowa Daga dogon tunanin da yayi Kallon little belah dake shakar bacci a kirjinshi yayi yanajin wani Irin zafi a zuciyarsa hawaye Duk sun wanke Masa fuska ya Bata peck a Ka ya ce "Mamanki ta na sonki Sosai my belah tun kafin kizo duniya ta Dora soyayyarki a ranta Saidai Ashe Allah baisa Zaku rayu da ita ba ,na tabbata Da Kinga gata da tsantsar Soyayyar da ba kowace uwace zatayiwa yarta haka ba Saidai Kash Mutuwa tayi Mana yankan kauna Zan Baki kulawa da Soyayya ta uwa Da ta uba inshallah bazaki Taba kukan maraici ba my belah I love you so much Ina sonki Sosai Kamar yanda nake kaunar Mamanki".

Yana ta tunannikanta bayan kuma ya bita Da Addua wajen 3:am barawon bacci ya sacesa a cikinsa ta kwana har safe.

Lokacin da suka Tashi momy Harta Tafi wajen Aiki dan yasan mommy ta na muhimmanta aikinta babbar likitan ce a private hospital Dinta na kanta babbar likita ce da ake matukar ji Da ita a Kasar Nan dama ketare saboda Macece marar Wasa akan komai.

Lokacin da ya shirya ta Tashi wanka yayi Mata Ya shiryata Bayan ya Bata abincinta Wasa ya dinga Mata ya na sata Dariya a palourn Sai Dariya take ta nazillo sosai suka shagala a Haka har momy ta dawo a aiki Murmushi Tayi ta na kallonsu gwanin burgewa ta ce

 "Aikin Keenan"Momy sannu da dawowa "yawwa Kai Kuma sannu da zaman raino"Ta fad'a Cikin zolaya"D'an Murmushin gefen Baki yayi da ba Kowa zai fahimci da Hakan ba Amsar Bela tayi ta ce "I miss You Belahn momy yau bamu gaisa ba To ga tsarabanki"ta fad'a ta nafiddo Katuwar sweet tare da sa Mata. A Baki sai Tanda take ta na Dariya Tana yiwa momy zillo Dakinta tayi Da ita dan har ya shige Ciki ajeta tayi ta dinga Wasa Taje ta watsa ruwa Taci Abinci kafin tazo ta kwanta D'anta huta Wanda Duk gwalgwalantun Bela ya hanata baccin sai chan itama Belahn baccin ya dauketa a Jikin mom da ta gangara mom ta Fara Tashi Bayan la'asr Murmushi Tayi ganin yanda yarinyar ta kafe jikinta ta na bacci a Hankali ta gyara Mata Kwanciyarta tare da rigarta dan ana sanyi ta Bata peck a kumatu ta Mike tayi toilet....✍️

"Naji Nasan duk wannan din habiby Amma kuma Kana da maganin Hakan?Koko duk halin Nan daka shiga ya dawo make da nabilar".


Momy nabila fa Bata mutu ba Wlh tallahi da idanu na Na ganta ki Yarda Momy Mata ta bata mutu ba".


 "to naji Aliyu Bata mutu ba shikenan Amma to ka nutsu Ka dawo hayyacinka sai Kaga Ta dawo gareka kilama Hada halin Nan daka shiga yasa Taki dawowa".


Cikin sauri ya kalli Momy ya ce"ko Momy"gyada Masa Kai Tayi ranta fall da tausayinta Dan nata.

 "Yana maraircewa ya Kama hannayen Momy ya ce "to Momy me zanyi my soulmate ta dawo kinsan ina tsananin kaunarta".


"nutsuwa zakayi ka koma asalin Aliyu na ka kwantar da hankalinka,Ka nutsu Ka dawo da farincikinka na baya Ka manta da duk wani Abu da ya Faru na bakin ciki a baya Indai kayi haka to Ni da kaina Zan taimaka maka wajen nemanta".


"Cikin sauri ya ce "na Yarda Momy zanyi"Murmushi Ta sakar Masa ta na lakutar kumatunsa ta ce.

 "good boy"Murmushin da ta manta yaushe rabon da ta gani a fuskarsa yayi ya na ikwancewa kafadarta ya ce "I love you so much first love "love you too my boy ta fad'a ta na Sake lakutar kumatunsa


 "kamar karamun yaro ya shagwabe fuska ya ce "Momy Even now you take me as a little boy after I grow up Gashi hada y'ata"Dariya kadan Momy Tayi ta ce

 "Ai kaidinne baka sanin Ka girma ka kusa Zama siriki"Dan Murmushi Tayi ya ce "Nan Gaba Muma zamuga girman Belah ko Momy".



 "insha Allah muje Yanzu Kaci Abinci "ta fad'a ta na Mikewa tanajin Dadi aranta yaushe rabon da taga farinciki a fuskar Dan nata haka.

Saidai me wata sabuwa da ya bullo washegari Tunda safe yayi Shiri Wai ya Tafi neman Belah Momy Ba Irin Yanda Batayi dashi ba ya Kiya Karshe sai Tare da little Belah ta Tafi wajen Aiki ta na Addu'ar Allah ya tsaresa a duk inda Yake

Yanzu abunda yafi damun Momy Keenan kwana Biyar sai dare Yake dawowa Tayi Fadan Harta gaji ta sawa sarautar Allah Ido Saidai kullum taje da belah wajen Aiki ga Bata Yarda da mutane Dole Saidai ta na hannunta ko Kuma ta Baza Mata Kayan Wasa kasa take samu ta gabatar da ayyukanta.

ta fad'a ta na nuna Mata a waya"kallon hotunan Tayi gwanin burgewa ta ce "wow Amma wajen ya burgeni zanso in ta'ba zuwa ko sau Daya ne".

 "Da Dy dad y "Da murnarta "sai sannan ya BUDE Ido ya na saukewa a Kan na farincikin nasa ware Mata Hannu yayi ya ce "oyoyo Daddy's princess"Yana daukarta ta hau Murna Murmushin yayi ya na lakutar kumatunta ya ce.

 "Khalifa Ne ya ce "Aunty Mu ki Fara Kaimu Park dinmu kafin Kuje chan"Ai kunsan wajen sai Kuje da kanku"To Ai kudinmu nawajenki"Cirar kudi Tayi ta basa ta ce "oya ka Kama hannun faruq Karkuyi wani wajen muna fitowa zamu sameku a ciki

"mu Raba hanya KE kiyi chan Ni inyi chan saimu Nemo su"to kawai Nasren ta CE ta na nufar Hanyar Da ta nuna Mata ta na cikin tafiya Taji tayi Karo da mutum Gaba Daya wayar dake Hannunsa ta Fadi cikin sauri ta dukursa da niyyar dauko Masa taji Ya rigata daukowa Saurin Dagowa tayi Daidai idonsa A kanta Gaba Daya idanunsu suka sartse cikin na juna wanda a take zuciyoyinsu suka buga tsorata da Irin kallon da Yake Mata ya sanyata Saurin cewa CIKIN rawar Murya,


"kayi Hakuri ban kula ba "ta na niyyar Barin wajen Taji an Janyo Gyalenta ta baya juyawar da zatayi taga Kyakyawan yarinyar Dake Hannunsa ta rikota ta na mika Mata hannu haka kawai taji yarinyar ta shiga ranta Hannu ta Mika Ta amsheta Shiko ya na tsaye kamar statue ya kasa Koda motsi sai idonsa dake kanta.

Bayan ta samu belah tayi bacci tagumi tayi ta na tunanin me Yasa Aliyu Yake cewa ya na ganin belah da chan shekara Guda da rasuwarta ko mafarkinta Bai ta'ba cewa yayi ba ya akayi da chan Batayi Masa gizau ba sai Yanzu?Kodai Aliyu ya samu Matsala a kansa NE?Sannan ta tuno Da maganar driver na yaga Belah a Hannun wata Bayan Kowa yasan Rashin yardar yarinyar ko Daukarta Ba ayi Amma Kuma Zanso ganin wanna yarinyar ko wacece Wacce sallau ya ce ya ga belah hannunta Dan maganar sallau ta Dauka serious hauka da dauki Ta Aliyu"

 a tare suka fito momy tayi serving dinsa Lunch Ya na ci ta na jansa Da Hira kadan kadan suna gamawa sukaji sallama' Momy ce ta ce "Waye"Nine"aka Fada Daga dayan bangaren "kallon Aliyu tayi ta ce.

"zuwan Nan naka NE marar kirki yaushe rabonka Da Gida na Idan ba ayi shekara Biyu ba antafi Ukku Dan Kai kanajin kanka ka Isa da kowa shiyasa Gaba Daya ka Raina Dangin ubanka To Bari kaji inma zuga ka ake ko kaki ko kaso dai mune dolenka dangi uba suke da karfi fiye da na uwa  ko a musulunci.".

Fada ya dingayi inda ya shiga Bata Nan Yake fita ba Abunda yaban mamaki ko Ke da Baki Nan Idan Dutse yayi magana to Aliyu ya tanka Masa Saida ya Gama fadansa kafin Kuma ya sauko ya ce"Haba Aliyu Kai d'aya Yaya Ya haifa a duniya Kai kawai muke.kalla Muji sanyi a ranmu meyasa kake Nesa Damu eyye Shifa zumunci ba Abun Wasa bane Ka dinga leka mu Dan Allah".


Sai sannan Aliyu ya Daga Baki ya ce"Insha Allah Kawu"Kafin yayi sama.yace Ina zuwa"Ba jimawa ya fito bandir din Yan dubu sabbi Guda Ukku ya Aje Masa ya ce "ba yawa dai Kawu "Girgiza Kai yayi ya ce "A a Aliyu bar kudin ka Ni zumunci ya kawoni kasan Banajin dadin Kana Nesa Da dangin mahaifinka sai naji kamar Yaya yabar Mana nauyi Ne mun kasa saukewa"


Kawu insha Allah Zan gyara Dan Allah Ka Amsa Daman nayi niyya Dakyal ya Amsa Kafin su koma hirar kasuwanci ya Dan Jima kadan Dan momy waje ta Basu kallon Belah yayi ya ce "Allah sarki marainiyar Allah Allah jikan mamanki Kafin ya kalli Aliyu ya ce "ya kamata kayi Aure kodan kula Da yarinyar Nan Aliyu Shekara Guda da Dori ba Mata ka samu Mata wacce zata lurar maka Da yarinyar Nan Duk ga ya'y'an Yan uwa Nan saika zaba Duk wacce kace kanaso baka ita zamuyi amma zaman Nan haka bana sonsa".



"wani iri yaji a Ransa Amma a fili ya ce "toh Kawu"A ranshi ya na tuna irin Dagun da Yake Sha Da Belah Akan Idan ya na janta zai Mata kishiya Har Rufe Kanta take a d'aki Taki budewa ta na Kuka ita Batason kishiya Dakyal yake shawo kanta ya na Mata Dariya ya ce"Wasa nake Miki my soulmate har abada KE Daya CE matar Aliyu'".


"Saurin goge hawayen da suke zubo masa yayi Yana dawo hankalinsa Jin Kawu na Masa sallama' har bakin mota Ya rakasa Kafin ya dawo ya na jinjina fitina irin ta kawu Umar Saidai abunda Yake burgeshi dashi Rashin son abin duniya Daman Su hud'u ne gidansu Abbanshi Abba ne Babba sai Kawu Umar sai Kawu Bala,Da Autansu Kawu Rabe dukkaninsu Kuma Da yayansu Amma Shi bayason shiga cikinsu sabanin Kawu Bala da ya Dora son duniya akan dukiyar gadon da aka bar Masa Dukda da kansa Yayi ihisani Lokacin da za ayi rabon gado Ya CE ya bar Musu karamun componyn Dady na noma Dake jigawa Dadai haka Tarkace banda tsabar kudi da akabawa kowanne su Amma har Yanzu Kawu Bala dukiyar A Idonshi Dan Kudin da ya B'ata a Rashin zuwanshi compony suna Da Yawa amma.baice masa Komai Dukda ya Sani Shi dukiya abunda ya dauketa Samamma ce kararrace Komai Yanda aka mutu aka bar Masa haka zai mutu ya Bari to Meye Abun hadama duka rayuwarma Barinta za ayi bare wata dukiya da Inda aka sameta za a barta Lokacin da ya koma itama momy Fadan tayi Masa Akan ya dinga shiga Yan uwan babansa Kar su kullaceta suce ita take hanashi.


Washegari kusan tattare suka fito Zasu tafi da momy Belah a hannunta cike da tausayawa ya kalli momy ya ce"gsky momy ya kamata a samu mai rainon nan.kina wahala da Yawa ga Aiki ga raino"No Ni bama wahalar yafi Damu na ba kawai yawan fita da ita din ga asibiti majalissar Kowa na zuwa shiyasa Amma zansa a Kara kawomun nanny konta sake yarda Da wata".


Washegari Momy tasa aka kawo Mata Yan Aiki su Biyar Amma Belah fur Taki yarda ko ta'ba ta suyi Ran momy badan yaso ba Ta kalli Mai kawo Mata Yan Aikin ta ce "kwangila ce na Baki Duk wacce Kika kawo Belah Ta yarda da ita kina Da kamashon Cash dubu Dari biyu 200k sannan Zan biya ta 150k a wata "laure na zaro Ido ta ce dubu dari da Hamsin hajiya Saikace Ma'aikaciyar jinya "ta na hade fuska ta ce.


"ina Fad'a ne ban Cika ba?cikin sauri ta ce "a a a hajiya Aike Kafin ki fadama kike cikawa Mai rainon hajiya karama kamar ansamu angama "Sauran da sukazo tare Duk sunyi tsuru tsuru yau suga samu sunga Rashi lura da haka yasa hajiya Suma Musu alheri Dukda ba haka suka so ba sunyi godiya Kafin su Tafi Laure ta na murnar Allah taimaketa ta samo Yar Aikin da zata Yarda Da Belah Yarinya Sai Fi'ilancin Jaraba Yar aiki ma Saita zaba Allahsa kartamun bakin cikin Dubu Dari biyun Nan Ina na ta'ba Kama dubu hamsin ma  Ita Kadai ta dinga zantuttukanta Tare da tafiya da niyyar Nemo wadanda ciki za a Dace..................✍️

Nasren Na zaune ta Gama sallar magrib Khalifa D'an Aunty ya zo ya ce taje abba na kiranta Saida gabanta ya Fadi Kafin ta Mike ta kalli mamanta da tayi tagumi ta ce "mama zanje Abba na kirana"a dawo lfy"Ta Fad'a tare suka koma Bayan ta Gaishe da Kowa Abba ya Amsa ya na washer Baki tare da cewa


 "Dalilin da yasa na Aiko a kiraki Nasreen Ina son ne kidawo Nan Da Zama Komai naki ya dawo Nan Ga d'aki chan spare ba Kowa Ya Zama mallakinki "ba nasren ba Kowa dake palourn saida ya Girgiza cike da bacin Rai mama Lami ta lafto wata ashar tare da cewa.



 "Ita Asuwa ?me kake nufi Wai Alhaji Me ka Hada da yarinyar Nan Dangin uwa ko na uba Eyye ka sanyata Makaranta Daya da diyanmu mun kyaleka Hakan Bai isheka ba Dole saika Chuso Mana Yarinyar nan marar asali gidanmu ta na Daga gefenma Ta na kawo Mana Matsala Ina Ga Kuma ta dawo kusa?".



cikin fushi Alhaji ya daka Mata tsawa ya ce "KE ubanme na hada dake?Gidanki Wai ko nawa ki dinga Magana gatsal Saikace gidan ubankine kema to nasren ta dawo Nan da Zama Inga ubanda zai Hana ki Tashi Kuban waje"Kowa Saida ya tsorata da Irin Yanda Alhaji yayiwa Mama itako Sosai ta shaku ta karajin linkin tsanar sabren a ranta ji take kama ta kashe yarinyar ta huta a fili ta furta.


 "Ni Alhaji Ni ka zaga a Saboda Wannan gantalalllar haduwar titi,Ni Lami Uwar yayanka Alhaji ka zaga yau Nagode"ta Fad'a ta na yin fuu tayi Gaba"tsaki ya ja ya na kallon nasren da takejin Tafi Kowa tashin Hankalin Hakan a nesa-nesa ma yan gidan Suna gallaza mata Ina ga kurkusa ya ce.


"Tashi gobe za a gyara Miki dakin Y'ata"To Abba"kawai ta iya Fad'a Kafin ta Mike a tsorace Gabanta na lugudan Faduwa in banda Batason Musu da Abba da Bazata amince da dawowarta cikin mutanen da ba kaunarta suke ba sannanma ya zatayi da Mamanta da ta Saba kwana jikinta Daidai ta Rana Daya Basu ta'ba kewar juna ba A haka harta Karasa Bq din ita kanta Mamanta Bata so haka ba Saidai Bata ta Yanda zatayi ne Taki nunawa nasren din Rashin Jin Dadinta Akan hakan.


Washegari Saida Abba ya sake aikowa Tukwana nasren ta koma cikin gidan banda Faduwa babu abunda Gabanta yake Dan harga Allah Mugun tsoron mama lami Da yaranta Take.


Kwanan nasren Ukku da dawowa cikin gidan saidai ta dukunkune a d'aki idan sundawo Daga school sury Kawai ke farinciki da dawowarta mama Lami ko dasun hadu harara ce Dan Alhaji ya Ce ta sake ta Daketa Ko yaji ta sake aibantata zai dauki mummunan mataki hakan yasa tabar komai a ranta Ashraf ko ta katsa ta tsare ko kadan Baisamu damar Da zai shiga Inda take ba Dukda Yanajin kewarta Sosai da ya na Murna ta dawo kusa dashi sai yaji ai gwara chanma Akan Nan din saukinta Daya Wajen Mamanta Take yini sai taga Abba ya kusa dawowa take Komawa dakin nata.

∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆

Gaba Daya da ka kalle yanayin fuskarsa kasan baya cikin farinciki dafoshi momy tayi ta ce "Kayi Hakuri habiby Tunda Yaya ya umarceka Da kaje Yana bukatar kane ko ka manta Yanzu Kaine kawai yake kallo matsayin d'a "momy Nifa Bawai naki zuwa bane Banason ne na Tafi ban Nemo Nabila ba Ina son shima nayi mashi albishir da ita shiyasa kawai"a ran momy ta ce "Daman Baka mance da Maganar Nan ba a fili kuwa cewa tayi"karka Damu habiby Kafin ka dawo na Nemo maka Ita Amma Ina son ka Tafi din kamar Yanda ya Bukata"Yana marairaicewa ya ce "promise fa momy"Ta na sakar Masa murmushin so ta ce "Insha Allah habiby"Hug Dinta yayi ya ce "Thank you so much first love ".


Yau takama Tafiyar Aliyu Egypt momy tayi Masa rakiya Har Airport tare da Belah da yakejin zaiyi kewarta Ta Musamman Saida momy ta ga tashin Jirginsu kamin ta dawo Gida ta na Masa Addu'ar sauka lafiya.

Abba......Kara rufe Mata Baki yayi ya ce kiyi a Hankali nasren "ta na fasa kuka ta CE "ka Fita Dan girman Allah Abba na shiga ukku Abba ko Kayi mantuwa ne Nasreen CE fa yarka"Cikin Wace Irin Murya ya ce"ba laifi na bane Wlh laifin zuciyata ne da ta kasa control din kanta a kanki Nasreen Tun Lokacin Da na Fara arba dake Allah ya jarabceni Da Muguwar Kaunarki Duk yanda Zan kwatanta Miki Irin son da nake Miki Nasreen wlh nayi karya,Ina sonki son da ban Tab'a yiwa wani mahaluki irinshi ba ,Zan iya Komai a kanki Nasreen Dan girman Allah ki Aure Ni wlh Zan iya Mutuwa idan Baki Aure Ni ba".


"rumtse Idonta sosai nasren tayi ta na Jin kalamar Abba kamar digar darma Cikin Wace Irin Murya Ta takaici ta ce"Abba ka manta da wacce Kake tare Ni kake Fadawa Kalmar so  matsayin uba fa na dauke ka?Wannan din ba Komai bane nasren Soyayya ba ruwanta da babba ko yaro wlh da gaske nake aurenki zanyi nasren idan kin amince juma'ar nan ma a Shirye nake a D'aura mana Aure idan ban aureki ba Zan iya Mutuwa saboda son da nake Maki ya zarce misali".

 "Ta na Mikewa Cike da takaici ta nuna mai hanya ta ce"Fita Abba ko nayi maka ihu tun muna mu Biyu Kafin Kowa yasan halin da ake ciki"ya na Shirin riko Hannunta ta fusge tare da fasa Kara ai Kafin ka kifta Ido Abba har ya bar dakin Kuka ta fashe dashi ta na hade kanta Da gwuiwa wannan Wace Irin rayuwace Wai ita Abba zai duba yace Yana so Bayan matsayin uba take kallonsa sannanma yasan Sarai Danshi yake sonta Bata Gama wannan tunanin ba Taji duk anshigo dakin Kallonta Momy da Sury da  ihun ya tadosu Dan mama Lami sunaji sukai burus a tare sukace.



 "lafiya dai ihunki muka jiyo"Gabanta na Faduwa ta kallesu Ta na shsheka ta ce"mafarki ne nayi Daman shine na tsorata"Duk ajiyar zuciya suka sauke Aunty ta ce"Addua yakamata Kiyi ba ihu ba Kiyi Addua ki koma bacci sannan karki fadawa Kowa Koma wani Irin mafarkine kikai Saida safe Ta Fad'a ta na yin Gaba ".


"sury zata Fita ta kamo Hannunta ta ce"Dan Allah ki tsaya mu kwana tare wlh tsoro nake ji"Ga alamun kin tsorata a mafarkinnan sosai badamuwa to "ta Fad'a ta na Kwantawa gefenta sai sannan ta danji sassauci tunani cinkushe a kwalwarta sai Wajejen 3 bacci yayi awon Gaba Da ita.



Washegari haka ta Tashi sukuku Ko A Makaranta Batayi karatun kirki ba Kallo Daya zaka Mata kasan akwai muhimmin abunda KE damunta Daman Bata da wata kawa da take sharing problems da ita mamantace babbar kawarta da take Fad'a Mata Komai nata Saidai Kuma a wannan Karin batajin ko ita zata fadawa Wannnan al'amarin da Yazo Mata a Bazata.


Koda ta dawo school bangaren Mamanta ta nufa Dan tayi niyyar Bazata koma ba Kallonta mama tayi ganin Duk tayi sanyi ta ce "nasren lafiya Naga Kin chanza"ta na kwancewa jikinta ta ce "bakomai mama"Bayan Isha i nasren harta kwanta khalifa yazo ya ce"Aunty nasren Abbanmu yace ki dawo za a rufe Gida"tanajin haushin Abba a ranta ta ce "Nan Zan kwana yau"Mamanta tayi Saurin harararta ta ce "Alhajin zai Aiko a kiraki kice Baki Zuwa wuce kiban waje"ta na shagwaba fuska ta ce "mama"Daga Mata hannu tai Wanda ba a son ranta ta bi Bayan khalifa ba.
Daren ranar ma Saida Abba Yazo Mata Yana Mata sambatun maganganu Shima Saida ta kusa Masa ihu Kafin ya Tafi.


Tun daga ranar nasren Bata da walwala Dan kullum Abba sai yazo Mata ya na sambatun Irin son da Yake Mata kwata-kwata a halin Yanzu Hankalinta a tashe Yake Gashi Mama Taki yarda Ta dawo wajenta Saboda kimar Alhaji da take gani nasren har wata Rama tayi Saboda Rashin isashshen bacci Dan kullum da fargaba take kwana Yauma Ta na Zaune Bayan Isha'i taji anturo kofar cike da tsoro ta Mike a zatonta Abba ne juyowa yayi ya na Hade hannayensa ya ce


"sorry wife na tsorataki nine"ya Fad'a ya na Karasowa bakin gadon ya zauna"cike da fargaba ta ce "Nidai Ka Tashi karkaja A ganka Ayi Mana wata fassarar Dan Allah Kar kajamun Matsala"calm down wife Basunan Daga Mama har Abba sunje dubiya kinsan kwanannan Nayi juriyar kewarkifa"Yana ganin yanda ta sauke nauyayyar ajiyar zuciya Ta ce"Ina yini"Hira ya dinga janta Da ita Tare da Bata wayarshi Tayi kallo tuni ta wartsake har Batasan dare yayi ba Saboda dauke Mata Hankali da Kallon yayi Kamar d'aga sama suka ga an banko kofar ba nasren ba Hatta Ashraf Saida ya tsorata mama Suka ga tsaye tare da Abba tsawa Mama ta daka Masa ta ce


 "Dan ubanka Daman baka Rabu da Shegiyar yarinyar Nan ba Shine Har kake Lallabowa So kake ta Bata maka tarbiya na rantse da Allah Ashraf baka Rabu da yarinyar Nan Komai Zan iya maka Kafin ta maida Kallonta ga Nasren data tsure ta ce "To farar munafuka ta Nan Kuma Kika bullo ,to wlh ki Sani Ashraf yafi karfinki yanda Jirgi yayiwa Keke tazara To haka D'ana Yayi Miki Kinyi maitarki ki Gama Amma Dana yafi karfinki banza marar Asali da galihu irinki ki cigaba Da nacewa Dana kiga Abunda Zan Miki Yar matsiyata".


Ta na Maganar ne cike Da tsantsar Masifa Abba ko Shima Masifar ce take cinsa Baisan Sanda ya gansa Tsakiyar dakin ba Daga hannu yayi ya Shararawa Ashraf Mari Kowa dake wajen Saida ya zaro Ido Har Aunty Dake Shigowa Karon farko Da Abba ya taba Marin Ashraf Dan kaf Cikin Yayanshi shine Star dinsa Kafin yayi wani yunkuri ya Kara shararesa da Mari Cikin Tsananin bacinrai ya shakosa ya CE "Dan Ubanka me Nace make game da Ganinka Da yarinyar Nan ,ban rabaku ba Watau ka nuna ban Isa dakai ba To kaji na rantse wlh wlh na kusa tsine maka Akan Baka Rabu Da nasren ba Gwara kabi duniya na Daina ganinka stupid".

 "Kafin Ya juya wurin nasren ya daka Mata tsawa ya ce"KE Kuma tunda bakijin magana Nasan Meye maganinki"Mama CE ta ce "Haba Alhaji Kuma saika Maresa a gaban kaskantacciyar yarinyar Nan fisabillahi dukda Nasan ciwon da kakeji Dan inaji nafika jin Haka Amma ai Kai kaja Ka koresu Mana Dasu asirce Mana d'a y'an Aiki gasu Nan burjik A gari"tsawar da ya daka Mata CE ta sanyata hadiye Sauran magananganunta hanya ya nuna Mata ya ce"Get out stupid"Ba Shiri Duk suka sabe har Aunty Da Bata karasoba Daman Ashraf ko Cikin Tsananin bacin rai ya ce"Abba Ni ka Mara Dan kawai Ina son nasren Abba meyasa bazakumin adalci ba ku tayani son abunda nakeso wlh Abba Ina son nasren Meye aibunta Dan nasota?Tsawa Abba ya daka Masa ya ce"Get out my friend"ya Fad'a Yana rumtse Ido"Cikin Tsananin bacin rai Ashraf ya fita har baya Ganin gabansa Saida yaga fitarsa Kafin ya koma wajen Nasren Ya na riko gashinta da tayi parking ta baya ya ce.


"meyasa kikeson azabtar Dani Da soyayyarki nasren kinsan Irin zafin radadin da nakeji idan na ganki da wani namiji kuwa,wlh ji nake kamar na kashe kaina Saboda Tsananin kishinki da nake ,Dalilin da yasa na killaceki a Gida Ko Ma'aikatan gidana maza banason suna ganinki,Ina son Na Gina Miki rayuwa Mai inganci,Amma kin tsaya Kina sakarci me zakiyi da wani Ashraf ga ubanshi Mai dukiyar gidan Gaba Daya Nasren Ina sonki Kuma saina Aure ki Dole wlh Banga mahalukin da ya Isa Ya hanani Aurenki ba a Halin Yanzu bank'i Kowa yasan Irin Soyayyar da nake Maki ba Dan Wataran gawata Za a tarar sonki da kishinki na iya hallakani"

Kukan ta ta karawa volume ta ce "Ni wlh Abba bana sonka bazan aureka ba Ni Kallon uba nake maka "idanunsa harsun sauya tsabar bacin Rai ya ce "Tamkar dirar darma Haka naji kalmar bana sonka Daga bakinki ,Ni Kuma Ina sonki Kuma dole na Aure ki  nasaka Ranar Aurenmu sati Biyu masu Zuwa zanga ubanda ya Isa ya Hana Kuma ki sake Bari na ganki da Ashraf kiga abunda zai faru'"

fuuu ya fita ta na ganin fitarsa ta Mike ta na Kuka Tayi waje Aunty da KE Zaune a palour tayi jugum ta binta da wani Irin kallo da gudu ta Fad'a dakin da Mamanta take harta Fara bacci ta hayeta ta na sakar Mata kukan da Bata Karasa sakinsa ba Hankali tashe mama ta ce "subahanallah nasren lafiya meya Faru"Ta na shsheka ta ce "mama Dan Allah mubar gidan Nan"Ta na Binta da wani Irin Kallo ta ce "Kamarya mubar gidan Nan nasren gidan da suka tallafemu suka inganta Miki rayuwa Gidan da Suka Mana Abunda Danginmu suka gagara Mana zakice mu Bari muje ina to?tukunama a wani dalili"?


ta na Kuka ta ce "mama Allah ya na sane Damu shizai cigaba Da tallafa Mana Nidai wlh mubar gidan Nan "a wani dalili"ta na shsheka ta ce "Mama Wai Abba ne Yace yana Sona zai Aure Ni Kuma wlh Mama Daman Duk dare saiyaje Yana cewa Yana Sona Nidai wlh mubar Musu gidansu,"Tana fiddo Ido waje ta. CE"so fa nasren kinsan abunda kike cewa kuwa?Alhajin ne zaice ya na sonki bayan matsayin y'a yake daukarki"Banason karya nasren idan kina so mubar Gida kuma saiki Masa sharri"ta na Kuka ta ce.


""Allah mama ban Masa sharri ba Sannanma duka sun rabamu da Ashraf Nidai Mu Tafi kawai"ta na harararta ta ce"Yanzu naji Batu watau sun rabaki da dansu shine Zaki Masa sharri ko nasren yaushe Kika Iya sharri haka bansaniba eh to wlh tun wuri ki bude kunnanki da kyau kiji Babu Inda xamu Daga Nan gidan Bamu da wajen Zuwa "Tana kuka ta ce "wlh Mama ba sharri nake Masa ba"Duk yanda taso tayiwa mama Bayani kiyawa tayi ta ce sharrine zata Musu "tare suka kwana Saida tayi bacci Tukwana mama tayi tagumi ta na nazarin maganganun nasren tabbas Indai Hakan ta faru tabbas akwai babbar Matsala sai Kuma wata zuciyar tace haba karya ne Taya alhaji zaiso nasren Bayan matsayin Diya ya dauke ta ko ita alhaji yace Yana so bare nasren Yar Cikin sa........✍️

🅿️...............*19 & 20*

Washegari nasren ta fito da shirinta na Tafiya Makaranta Abba ya fito Rai hade ya Ce koma Ciki ba inda Zaki ".

"ta na narai narai da fuska ta ce"Abba Makaranta fa CE zani"Nafiki Sanin hakan kin Gama makarantar waec and neco ma zansa a zana Miki ".

"Da gudu ta koma ta na Zuwa ta fada Cikin mama ta na Sakin Kuka ta ce"mama Abba yace ya cireni a makarantar mama Dan Allah ki mashi magana yayi Hakuri ya maidani wlh inason karatun sosai Saida aski Yazo gaban goshi Komai zai jagule mun ".


"Gaban mama na Faduwa ta nanata innalillahi wa inna ilaihiraji'un a fili Bata nunawa nasren din damuwarta ba sai Lallashinta tayi ta ce"Kiyi Hakuri zai hakura Bar kukan Yar lelena"haka mama tayi ta Lallashinta.


Wajejen 1pm nasren ta fito compound tayi tagumi Dan mama ta na ciki ta na Aiki Aunty da ta fito hartayi Gaba sai Kuma Ta dawo ta ce"Lafiya bakije makarantar ba"Tanaji kamar ta fasa Kuka ta ce "Abba ne ya CE ya cireni"Tofa wata sabuwa Allah kyauta"kawai ta fada tanayin Gaba Abunta.


Nasren da kallo ta bita tazata zatace' zata Basa Hakuri wannan Wace Irin rayuwace Ta na Nan Zaune har Ashraf yadawo d'aga office Nan ya sameta kamar yanda Ta fadawa aunty haka ta fada Masa ta na hawaye ta ce


"Dan Allah ka basa Hakuri ya maidani"shiru Ashraf yayi ya na nazarin Abun Kafin ya sauke ajiyar zuciya ya. Ce"karki Damu wife kidaina Kuka Dole Zaki koma Makaranta duka wata nawa ya rage ki kammala zaice ya cireki"Lallashinta ya dingayi har ya samu tayi shiru tanajin dad'i a ranta.


Misalin goman dare Ashraf ya Samu Abba Gaba Daya suna palourn da Maganar komawar sabren Makaranta Hade fuska sosai Abba yayi ya CE.


"na Cire ta din ko da chan Kai kasanya Na sanyata Tukwana ma Wai ban rabaka da ita ba Ta Ina kasan na cireta eh So kake muyi mugun sabawa dakai a gidan Nan Wlh Zan iya Maka Komai akan baka Rabu da yarinyar Nan ba ,Ina gab da koranka a gidan nan".


"ya Karasa fada ya na Nuna sa Da yatsa"Cikin bacin Rai hajiya Lami ta ce "Wai Kai Alhaji saika dinga wani Zazzakewa Da tada jijiyoyin wuya Akan shegiyar yarinyar zaka Kora danka,meye na wani haihakewa Ko aurenta zakayi Wai musan da wata a kasa".


Eh Aurenta zanyi sonta nake ko cikinku Akwai ubanda Ya Isa Ya Hana?Kowa dake wajen Saida ya Girgiza dajin Maganar da ta fito daga bakin Abba Ashraf na Mikewa ya ce"Abba Dan Allah ka Daina Mana Irin wannan Wasan bai kamata ba"

Ba Wasa nake ba Da gaske nake Nasren aurenta zanyi sati Biyu masu Zuwa Dan haka Kuma fara Shirin tarbar sabuwar Amarya ko Haram ne Dan na aureta Eyye kukayi mun wani jugum-jugum"Ashraf Da yakejin Zuciyar sa kamar zata bar gangar jikinsa Baisan Lokacin da ya juya ba ya fita Har baya ganin gabansa"


Wata uwar ashar Hajjiya Lami Ta Lafta ta ce "lallai Alhaji Watau Yanzu na gano Kan Zaren Kana nufin wannan Tsinanniyar yarinyar zaka Hada matsayina da nata Idan ka Isa Allah tsinan wlh baka Isa Ka Auri yarinyar Nan ba na yarda ka Auri Mata sama Da dari Amma Banda yarinyar Nan Wlh kaiko Kunya Bakaji Gaban y'ay'anka da Kowa ka Furta Kalmar Nan Ka rasama wacce zakaso Sai budurwar danka tirrr wlh Kuma saina yiwa yarinyar Nan lilis Wlh sai sun bar gidan Nan Tunda ba na ubansu bane".

"KE Dayake gidan Ubanki ne ba Lami Ki daki yarinyar Nan Ki daki igiyar aurenki wlh Kika sake wani Abu ya sameta Akanki zai Kare"Fada Lami Da Abba Sukayi sosai Wanda Aunty koke da bakinan Dukda Maganar ta soketa Amma A zuciyarta tabar Abun Daman ta jima da Ganewa Alhaji Kanshi Yake ma Fada Yaranko ba Wanda baiji bakin Cikin Wai babansu zai Auri nasren Har sury Da suke Shiri Saida Taji nasren din ta fita a ranta.


.washegari karfe bakwai Batayi ba su nasren sukaji Bugawar kyauran dakin su Da karfin gaske Ba Shiri suka Mike Kafin suyi wata wata an banko kofar Gaba daya Yan matan gidan ne tare da Lami a gaba idan aka cire Yaran Aunty Dukansu Kan nasren Sukayi suka rufeta da duka.

 Mama Kuka ta fasa ta na rikesu ta ce"me Y'ata tayi Muku da Zaku hallakamun Ita me mukayi Muku?mama Lami na huci ta ce"bakaken munafukai watau Daman A munafurce kuke Zaune damu amma ai laifinmune Daman ance tsintacciyar mage Bata mage Watau an samu waje har kike Shirin Aurawa mijina shegiyar yarki mun Muku Rana ku Mana dare ko? to Bari kiji na rantse da Allah Auren y'arki da Alhaji Tamkar Ranar Mutuwar y'arki ne Tun wuri Ki tanadi likkafani da tabarmar kaba Dan nafi krgin nayi kishi da banzar yarinyar Nan Taki Mai Ido ya na mujiya,"

"Gaba Daya Kan mama ya kulle Kuka take Tana turesu subar Dakar Mata 'ya Kafin ta kalli mama Lami ta ce"Dan girman Allah da darajar fiyayyen halitta Ki Musu magana karsu Halaka mun y'a na yarda kome kikeso zamuyi Amma karku illatamun y'a".


"wani shu'umin murmushi mama Lami tayi ta ce "Ku dakata "Saida suka Kara jibgarta suka kyaleta Da gudu Mamanta ta rungumota Dukansu Kuka suke ba karamar jigatuwa nasren tayiba tunda take ba a Tab'a dukanta haka ba Jikin Mamanta ta shige ta na shsheka.


"idan Kina son Cigaban Rayuwar y'arki to ya Zama Dole yau yau dinnan ku bar gidan Nan,KUYI nesa Damu ku tattara ku koma inda Kuka fito idan kukayi haka Kun taimaki kanku Kun rufawa kanku asiri idan ba haka ba wlh kinji na rantse Saina Zama ajalin y'arki"Hankali tashe mama ta ce.


"Zamu bar gidan Nan kamar yanda Kika Fada Nasren ta fiyemun Komai da Kowa duniyar nan Amma Ina son ki Sani Arziki Da Rashin sa duk na Allah ne ba asan me gobe zata haifar ba mungode da Irin taimakonku garemu Allah saka da Alkhairi".


"Kafin ta kalli nasren Dake jikinta ta ce "Tashi mubar Musu gidan su nasren"Daka kalli fuskar mama kasan ta na Cikin Tsananin fushi A gaggauce ta hada Musu Komai nasu a ghanar su Suka Sanya Hijab ta Kama Hannun yarta Suka fito".

Gud'a Lami ta rangad'a ta ce "Allah raka Taki gona sai a tari wani sarkin ass tsiya tabar Mana Gida"girgiza Kai kawai mama ta tayi ta na goge hawaye ta ce"hmmm Kaico rayuwa Allah Bamu Ikon cinye jarabawowin Nan"


Saida Sukayi nisa da gidan sosai Kafin su zauna su huta nasren na shigewa Jikin mama ta ce"Ki kwantar da hankalinki mama Allah Yana tare Damu Ni farinciki ma nayi da barin mu gidan Dan Allah karki Damu kinji mamana Kinsan KE Kadai Nake dashi"ta na Kallonta ta ce .


"Dole na Damu nasren Ina kike tunanin zamuje Yanzu Bamu da wurin zuwa "ta na goge hawaye ta ce"Mama mu koma kazaure Mana"girgiza Mata Kai tayi ta ce "Bazamu koma Gida ba nasren Ina son Nayi nesa dasu kamar yanda suka bukatar basa kaunar ganin mu na tozartar dasu na Bata Musu sunan zuri'a Ina son rayuwarki ta inganta kamar ko Wace y'a Amma Kuma Kash Kaddara ta rigayi Fata Ashe gaskiya kike fadamun Da gaske Alhaji zai iya cewa Yana sonki Gaba Daya Rayuwar tazomana a baibai sai Allah ya taimakesu Kaddara ta risko"

Tana Goge Mata hawaye ta ce"Kiyi Hakuri  ki Daina Cewa haka mama Dan Allah ki manta Komai Indai inaganin kukanki Nima Zan iya shiga wani Hali"Gyada Mata Kai tayi ta ce"Allah Muku albarka nasrenTana murmushi ta ce"Ameen mamana"Saida suka Gama hutawa Kafin suyi Gaba Tafiya suka dinga sha Batare da sunsan Inda Zasu nufa ba nasren Duk ta Gaji  Har Isha'i suna Gararanba Yanzu babban tashin Hankalinsu Wajen kwana sunsan yanda Rayuwar Nan take da Rashin yarda ba kowanne zai tallafemu  ya Basu masauki ba".

dakalin wani gida suka Zaune suna sauke gajiya pure water da awarar da mama tasai Mata a hanya ta sinta ta Fara ci kamar wacce Bata tabacin Abinci ba Ta na Gama ci ta kwance cinyar mama baccin gajiya ya kwashe ta Kallonta mama ta dingayi ta na tuna rayuwa Yanda tayi da ita tabbas Rashin uwa babban tashin Hankali ne D'anta tabbatar da mahaifiyarta na Raye da bazata shiga halin Nan Da take ciki ba Haka Mahaifinta adduar Allahkawo Musu mafita kawai take.


Kamar d'aga sama Taji an dallosu Da fitila a tsorace ta firgita ta na kallon Yar Dattijuwar da ta fito Zata kulle Gida ta na Dan lekowa waje ta ce "bayin Allah Lafiya kuke Zaune Nan dare ya kusa latsawa"lura da matar kamar ta na da mutunci ya Sanya mama cewa "Dan Allah taimako muke nema Da wajen Kwana Kafin gobe Mu Kara Gaba mu Baki NE"ta Jima ta na kallonsu Ta na nazarin Maganar mama Tare da dallesu da fitila a Zuciyar mama Addua take Allah sa ta amince Chan Taji ta ce


 "Badamuwa ku shigo Dukda duniya ta baci Mutane ba Abun yarda bane Kayi Musu Rana su maka dare haka nandai zanyi shahada ku shigo nayi Dan Allah"Cike da farinciki mama Ta ce"nagode Allahsaka da Alkhairi Kafin ta tada nasren dake Cikin gigin bacci suka shiga madaidaicin gidan na kasa Wani daki ta nuna Musu ta ce.

 "Ku shiga Nan dakin ku kwanta Saida Babu katifa Amma akwai tabarma"godiya sosai mama ta sake Mata suka shiga dakin Wanda suna shiga nasren ta baje Kan tabarmar ta na Kwanciya Mama ko Bata iya bacci Tunanin Mutane Biyu masu matukar mahimmanci A Cikin rayuwarta Ta dingayi sai Gabannin asuba baccin Wahala ya dauketa cike da mafarkai barkataiii.......✍️

Hankali kwance nasren ta Tashi lura da mama Batayi baccin kirki ba ya sanyata Kyaleta Tsakar gidan ta fito Bayan ta Gama alwala ta koma ta gabatar da Salla ta na Addu'ar Allah kawo Musu mafita.




Sai wajen 7 mama ta Tashi ta na Gama Salla Dattijuwar da ta saukesu ta shigo da fara'arta ta Ce "sannunku Ya bakunta"Gaisheta Nasren tayi Yayinda suka shiga gaisawa da mama ta na Kara Mata godiya ta ce.


 "Bakomai Bar gode mun saidai bansan sunankuba"Nuna Nasren mama Tayi ta ce"ita wannan sunanta Nasreen Ni Kuma suna na Asma'u"Washe Baki Tayi ta ce.


"Masha Allah nasmen "Yar dariya Nasren tayi ta ce"Nasren ne kaka"Ahto ban fada Daidai ba Keenan wannan sunan naki Mai wahalar Fad'a nikuma Suna Na Laure Ban taba Haihuwa ba wannan gidan ma Da nake ciki Na Haya ne D'an Yar uwata yake Biya mum Duk shekara".

 "murmushi mama tayi ta ce "Allahsrk Allah biyasa"Tabe Baki laure tayi ta ce "Ameen dai za ace Ai uwarsa Gida ya saya Masa tankamame "a Zuciyar nasren Ta ce"wannan tsohuwa da fitina take"Mama ta ce "Ai Rayuwar yanzunce haka take sai Hakuri".


aikuwadai Yanzu ka nemu na kanka ka dogara da kanka Shi yafi Kinganninan na Tafi shekara 70 Amma Banbar neman na kaina ba ,Duk buge-bugen da Nasan zanyi Na ciyar Da kaina Yi nake Amma sai nake ganin kamar ku Baki NE a garin Nan Naga hausarku ba irin tamu ba"


murmushi mama tayi ta ce"eh Mama mu Bak'i NE Amma gaskiya mun shekara ko Biyu a garin Nan"Allahsrk Niko zanso naji meya fito Dake Da Daren Nan Nasan dai ba qalau ba ".


a takaice mama Ta labartawa mama laure tun zuwansu Kano har abunda ya Faru ya barosu da gidan alhaji"Laure ta Tab'a hannu da salamcewa ta ce

 "Amma Anyi lalatacciya matar Nan Daman ance Bata da mutunci Ina Jin labarinta Ai idan nice KE Aura Masa ita zanyi inga ta tsiya"Girgiza Kai tayi ta ce "a a a zaman lafiya yafi zama Dan sarki"nasren ko a ranta ta ce"Kuji itama wannan tsohuwar inyi me dashi".


"Laure ta kame hab'a ta CE "To ita Ina Mahaifinta naji Duk labarin da kikaban banji na Mijinkiba"Mama najin kamar an tabo Mata wani rauni a zuciyarta ta D'aure ta ce"Mahaifinta Ya rasu tun ta na Yarinya".


 "Allahu Akhbar kaico Wlh harkun bani tausayi Allah Jikansa inshallah Indai zaku iya Zama Dani Ga Guri Nan muyi zaman mu Tare "Cike da murna Mama Ta ce.


"Amma munji Dadi mama mungode Allah saka Da Alkhairi"ta na Mikewa ta CE "ai yiwa Kaine Bari naje Na fita neman Yan Aikin Nan Naga yau ko a Dace Ga Karin kummallonku chan a tukunya nasmin Saiki zubo Muku'".


"ta na fita Nasren ta kalli mama ta na marairaicewa ta ce"mama kikace Babana ya mutu Bayan kema Kince bakisan Inda yake ba"To Ni kaina bansan Mahaifinki ya mutu ko Yana Raye ba nasren"Tana marairaicewa ta ce "inshallah mama Bai mutu ba Dan Allah ki Fadamun Waye Mahaifina".

"murmushin yake kawai tayi ta ce "Tashi kije ki zubo abincin muci".


Yau kwanansu nasren Biyar a gidan mama Laure tuni sun saki jiki Da ita kamar yanda ta saki jiki Dasu nasren ta dauketa kamar jika Yanzu babban abunda yafi tadawa mama Hankali yanda mama Laure Ke dawainiya Dasu Dukda tasan Bata nuna gajiyawarta ba Amma tasan Dole wataran ta gaji Gashi Yar dubu Biyun ma da ta rage mata sun cinye yauma suna Zaune mama Laure ta leko Ta CE


 "To nikuma na Tafi Idan ban dawo da wuri ba nasmen Ki Dora Mana girki Yau dai Ina fatan Allahsa na Dace da wadda zata yarda da kinibabbar yarinyar nan"Nasren CE ta ce"Wai Kaka Ina NE kike zuwa kullum dakin dawo saiki dinga mitar wata yarinya".


"Tana Jan tsoka ta CE "wlh samu NE na Gani nake neman rasa shi dubu dari Biyu Ce Nan Nake fafutukar nema"Nasren na zaro Ido ta ce"Kaka dubu dari Biyu Kuma?a Ina ake samu"gidan matar Da nake kaiwa Yan aikine to D'anta ne matarsa ta mutu Ta bar karamar yarinya ita Hajiyar Ma'aikaciyar jinya ce shima d'an ma'aikaci ne shine ake neman Mai kula da yarinyar amma bata yarda da kowa shine Hajiyar ta ce na kawo mata wacce ta yarda da ita zata bani dubu dari Biyu ita Kuma albashin Mai rainon dubu dari da hamsin a wata wlh Ina fada miki yarinyar Nakai mata sunfi a kalla ashirin amma Taki yarda da kowa Amma naki hakura Ina ta gwada sa'a ta ko Allah zaisa a Dace".


"Nasren na zaro Ido ta ce "Kaka dubu dari da hamsin albashi Saikace malamar asibiti"Kaka na wangale Baki ta ce"Wlh Yar Nan yosu a wajensu wannan Ba komai bane manyan masu kudi NE mashahurai Ina Baki labari"Jinjina Kai nasren tayi ta ce.

"wasu kudin kamar anyo yayinsu"Bari Karna biyeki na wuce "Kaka ta Mike"Nasren na Mikewa ta CE"Muje na rakaki"harta rakata bakin kofa ta na tunanin Dubu dari da hamsin din da Kaka ta fada Ranar yini tayi ta na tunanin ace Yarinyar Nan ta yarda da ita ita ko na wata daya ma suka Biya Mata burinta Ta koma Makaranta ta zana waec da neco ta sayawa mama Sabbin Kaya ,haka tayi ta wasikar jakkanta acewarta.


Washegari Nasren ta kasa Daurewa Tana cinyar Mamanta ta ce"Inama Yarinyar Nan ta yarda Dani mama Da kudin wannan Ana ban Zan koma Makaranta na saya Miki sabbin Kaya"murmushi mama Tayi ta ce"nasren kenan yaushe Kika Fara son kudi"ta na Yar Dariya ta CE "Mama Kawai Ina son Kiyi farincikine Nasan Kina son gayu samu ne bakyayi sannan kina son cikar burina na Zama cikakkiyar likita".


"Shafa kanta mama tayi ta ce "iNsha Allahu burinki Saiya cika Y'ata Allah Muku Albarka Yasa ku Gama Da duniya Lafiya"Ameen mamana Amma meyasa Inzakiyi mun Addua kike jam'i Bayan Ni kadaice y'arki"Murmushin yake kawai Mama Tayi batace da ita Komai ba itama Bata sake Wata Tambayar ba.


Yau Kaka ta dawo a gajiye sai mita Take ta na Wannan yarinyar da bakin ciki take Dole dai ta rantse bazanci kudin Nan ba Gaskiya inaga Nakusa hakura yo ko chachar kudin Abun Hawa ma ta isheni"Nasren dake gefenta a tabarma ta ce.


"Kaka Kina Ganin ko Muje Tare mu gwada sa'a kilan in muna Da rabo a dace'"Kaka na Kallonta ta ce "Amma jikata kina ganin Zaki Iya sannan mamanki Ta yarda"Kallon mama tayi taga Tayi kasa da kanta Kafin ta kalli Kaka ta ce.


"Indai mama Ta yarda mukaje Ai gwada sa'a ne Tunda Bai Zama lallai Ta yarda Dani ba"Kaka na Kallonta mama ta ce "Asma'u kina ganin ba matsalar muje"Dan ita Kaka Kyan nasren har tsoratata yake"Murmushin Yake mama tayi ta ce


 "Bakomai mama Allahsa a Dace"a ranta Tace bama ta yarda dake ba Dan Ni banason hulda da masu kudi Yanzu "Cikin murna Nasren ta ce "Yawwwa Mama ta yarda Allahsa ta yarda Dani"Kaka na Kurawa nasren Ido ta ce "iNsha Allahu Zama ta yarda dake nakeji a jikina,kinsan sainagama wlh kamar Kuna yanayi da yarinyar itama jawur Take kamar KE dinnan Sak balarabiya Dukda Dana tambayi mamanki ta ce Baku hada Komai da Larabawa ba ko Turawa Amma ita yarinyar Balarabiya ce gaskiya Gaba da baya Uwa da uba haka naji ance".


Nasren na zaro Ido ta ce"Kai ko shiyasa Bata yarda da Mutane,to Daman Akwai Larabawa a kasar Nan?ehmana Amma Basu fiya Zama ba Kije ki kwanta da wuri gobe ki Tashi da wuri"Tana murna ta ce.


 "to Kaka " Juyawa tayi Taga ba mama a gurin da saurin ta shiga dakin Zaune ta samtea Tana hawaye Wanda Tana ganinta ta maza ta boye Tana kirkirar murmushi a sanyaye ta Karasa ta ce.


"mama meyasaki Kuka ko bakyason naje wlh Indai kikace bakyaso na hakura"Tana girgiza Mata Kai ta ce"Ina so Mana Nasren Maza kwanta ba Kuka nake ba'"da Addu'ar Allahsa Yarinyar ta yarda da ita bacci ya dauke ta.......✍️

*WOMEN'S DAY*🦅🦅❤‍🔥


🅿️..............*23 & 24*


Washegari Karfe Tara tayi masu a bakin gate din Gidan su Aliyu' Zaro Ido sosai nasren tayi ta na Karewa tankamemen gidan kallon,Tunda take tsawon rayuwarta Bata taba ganin Gida Mai kyansa ba acewarta ko gidan su Ashraf da take ganin mugun kyanshi da taga wannan sai taga wancen Ashe ba komai bane Saida Mama laure ta Kamota Tadawo hankakinta.

 kauyanci tayi sosai Koda shigarsu farfajiyar gidan Wanda Saida Mama laure ta nuna takardar shaidarta na shiga gidan Kafin A barsu su shiga Kalle-kalle kawai take kamar wace sakarai mama laure ta ce.


"Kinga gidan ya had'u ko jikata"Murmushi tayi ta ce"Eh Kaka yayi kyau sosai kamar ba a duniya ba"Dariya mama Laure tayi ta ce "dukiya CE ayi ta tarata kamar ba a mutu ba "Shiru tayi Jin mama Laure tafara wasu zantattakun "a main Palour suka yada zango Wanda shigarsu ba jimawa Mai Aiki tafito ta ke shaida Musu Hajiyar ta na wajen Aiki Saidai Gobe ko anjima da yamma kudawo".


A ran naaren bataso ba taso taga wannan Wace hajiya ce mamallakiyar wannan tankamemen gidan har suka Isa Gida ta na santin gidan.

Koda nasren ta dinga bawa Mama Labarin gidan Da haduwarshi Saida jikinta tayi sayi Cikin hikima ta kalli mama Laure ta CE .


"Amma Mama Mijinta NE Mai gidan ko?Mama Laure na gyara Zama ta ce"Mijinta Yajima Da Rasuwa D'anta d'aya a duniya Sai jikarta Daya Da Allah Yayiwa Mamanta Rasuwa Dukda har matar tasa ta mutu bansanta ba saboda basu fiya Zama a kasar ba Yanzu haka d'an Yana chan kasar dangin uwar ko Yama sunan kasar na manta Amma dai Yankin Larabawa ne D'aga ita sai jikarta sai Yan Aiki Duk girman wannan gidan da kike gani mutum Biyu ne Yanzu cikinsa Hajiyar nada Wani Irin Hali Bata da Sakin fuska ,Batasan Wasa Da raini ko Kadai Saidai Kuma Akwai kirki amma Kuma Babu Sakin fuska Sannan ta na Tsananin kaunar Dan Nan nata Da jikarsa Koda Allah yasa Kin samu Aikin to ki kula sosai Dan Akan wannan tatsiyar yarinyar da kike gani sai a tada gari".


Ajiyar Zuciya mama ta sauke Daman so take Taji ba maza a gidan Dan batason nasren Na mu'amula Kusa da maza Sai Taji Aikin ya kwanta Mata arai Kari na farko da tayi Addu'ar Allah sa a Dace a dauketa Cikin murnar mama ta sauko nasren tace amen.

Washegari Karfe hud'u suka Isa gidan saidai me tun a bakin gate Security suka hanasu shiga Sukace hajiya ta ce Bata da bukatar ganin Kowa yau kowa ya zo Kar a barshi ya shigo"Cikin kufula Da Jin haushi mama Laure ta ce"Ai ita tace nazo tasan da Zuwa na kaje kace Mata Laure CE tazo da Mai rainon belah"Ya na rufe kofar ya ce"Ta CE Bata Da appointment da kowa Dan haka ku koma Gobe Kuna iya dawowa."


Tsaki mama Laure taja ta ce"Sai asarar kudin napep muke Nasren Ko ita kanta Taji haushi Tana murna Sai Gashi murnarta ta koma ciki Rai ba Dadi suka koma Gida.


Washegari Karfe Biyar suna Zaune a palourn hajiya Sunkusa Minti Talatin a Zaune amma basu fito ba nasren dai Kalle-kallenta kawai take ta na Shan Sanyi ac Mai hade kamshin Hadaddiyar freshener har wani lumshe Ido take Wani bacci na neman fisgarta a ranta ta ce"ni'imar duniya ma Keenan Ina ga ta aljanna Allah Bamu Aljanna(amen ya Allah)


Kusan Awa Guda kafin suga shigowarta Sakato nasren Tayi ta na Kallonta Balarabiya sak Ga hadaddiyar doguwar rigar atamfar kanta da Tasha Abun kalloce kunnenta Kare da latsetsiyar wayar Dukda nasren Batasan ta wayoyi ba Amma ta tabbatar wayar dake Hannun Hajiyar chan Ta haura sama da One million A ranta ta ce.


 "Duniya kenan Lokacin da wani KE Shan wuya Lokacin wani kejin Dadi "zauna Ta hakimce Tana ci Gaba Da waya batare da Ko ta kalli side dinsu ba ,Kasancewar da larabci Take Maganar yasanya Basu San me take cewa ba ,ta Kusa 30 minutes ta na waya Kafin ta aje Gaisheta Mama Laure tayi ta Amsa Ta na danna waya Gaban nasren na Faduwa ta gaisheta" kasa-kasa Shiru hajiya tayi Tana nazarin Ina tasan muryar Nan  dukda kasa-kasa tayi Maganar a takaice ta Amsa .


"Yawwa Hajiya sabuwar Mai raino ce na samo Ko Allah yasa a kanta a Dace "Dagowa tayi Ta na kallon Mama Laure ta ce "Ina ga na ma fasa Maganar nannyn Nan Tunda Tak'i yarda da kowa Sannanma mutanen Yanzu ma ba Abun yarda bane kawai kibar neman Kowa ma Na fasa zancigaba Da kula Da jikata har Allah ya nuna mana Girmanta Zanba Jamila Yanzu ta Baki 20k sai Kuma na neme ki".


ta na Gama fad'ar haka ta Mike ta na nufar stairs wasu hawayene masu zafin gaske suka shiga zubowa nasren A ranta ta ce "meya sa bani da sa'a akan koma Ne wai?..........✍️

🅿️.................*25 & 26*

Cikin ladabi mama Laure ta ce"ai ko gwadawa da Anyi hajiya Kilan a Dace kiyi Hakurin a gwada Akan wannan din idan Bata aminta ba shikenan".

"Kaka kawai Muje tunda Ba a Dace  ba Bama lallai kila ta yarda Dinba"Kasance da karfi tayi Maganar Ya Sanya Momy juyowa ta na tsaka Da Hawa stairs a ranta ta ce"Kamar Irin muryar nabila Nake ji Wannan Wace yarinyace?

Hakanan zuciyarta ta Bata Da ta juya ta kalli ko wacece "Juyawar da zatayi Daidai nasren na Mikewa ta na goge hawaye wani Irin faduwar Gaba Momy Taji wacce Bata Tab'a Jin Irinta ba tsawon rayuwarta Bata Tab'a ga Abunda Ya gigitata Tare da firgita ta ba kamar yau,Nabila Da ta mutu Ta dawo?

Ta fad'a a ranta Daidai nasren ta juya Na Shirin fita ai Batasan sanda ta sauko Da gudu ba ta Ce"Nabila "Kafin Kuma Ta ruga ta baya ta rungumota ta ce"Nabila Daman kina Raye Baki mutu ba?

?mamaki ya Gama cika nasren wace Nabila Kuma?ita Ina ta sansu Bayan Bata Tab'a ganinsu ba Ta na juyowa Cikin rawar Murya "Ta ce.

"Hajiya Ni ba sunana Nabila ba suna na Nasren"sakin rungumar Momy tayi Jiki sanyaye ta Kama Hannunta Kallonta kawai take tanaso ta karyata Idanunta Akan wacce suke Gani Amma har Yanzu Bata gaskata ba Nabila bace.

 Ita Abun tsoro ma Ya ba Nasren Gashi ta garkame Mata hannu kamar za'a sace ta kallon Mama Laure Momy tayi ta ce


"wacece wannan?Itama mama Lauren a tsorace take a takaice Taba Momy labarin haduwarsu dasu nasren din,Kara kallon nasren din Momy tayi taga sak nabilar ta Wata ijiyar zuciya ta sauke Mai nauyin gaske ta ce.


"Allah Mai iko?Allah Mai halittu Kala-kala Daman ance Halittar Mutu Irinsa Biyu ne bantaba ganin kamar da ta rudani ba irin ta wannan yarinyar da Nabila ta dole Aliyu' ya zauce ya ce,Nabila Bata mutu ba Keenan wannan ce Aliyu' Yake Gani matsayin Nabila har nake karyatashi inko Hakane akwai wata hikima Da Allah ya boye nayin haka Nabila ta Tafi wata nabilar Kuma ta b'ullowa Abun da matukar al'ajabi kamar almara?

"Duk Cikin zuciyarta Momy take maganar Nan"nasren dukta tsure lura da Hakan ya Sanya Momy sakin Hannunta ta ce.

"sorry ki zauna"Kuyi Hakuri kinmun Kama da wata Y'ata ce da ta rasu"A ran nasren ta ce"Allahsrk"a fili kuwa k'asa da kanta tayi"Zama Momy tayi Tana dafe kanta da ya Fara sara Mata Da ta dago saita kalli nasren ta na son ta tabbatar da ba Nabila bace Amma ta kasa ganin Koda wata alama ta banbancinta da ita ta tabbata ko Yan biyu Sukayi kamar Nan ta b'aci .

"Yama kikace sunan naki?Cewar momy ta na Kallonta har Lokacin"a Hankali ta ce"Nasren"Shine Real name dinki"?

Gyada mata Kai tayi ta ce "Eh"Numfashi taja ta ce

"Matakin karatu fa?Ta na Wasa da yatsun Hannunta Dan matar kwarjini take Mata ta ce.

"Ina s. S three na kusa kammalawa halin rayuwa yasa ban Karasa ba"a ran Momy ta ce "Allahsrk rayuwa ji kamar wannan yarinyar har tasan Tsananin rayuwa".

a fili kuwa cewa tayi "zamuyi Duk wannan Maganar later Bari na dauko yarinyar.

 "Ta fada ta na Mikewa mama Laure addua kawai take Allahsa a Dace nasren ko jikinta Duk yayi sanyi Har aikinma sai Taji ya Fara fita a ranta ba Ayi 5 minutes ba Momy ta fito rike da belah.

 "Dagowa Nasren tayi ta na kallon yarinyar da ta kasa mantata tun Lokacin haduwarsu Da ta kirata da Momy ta na d'an zaro Ido Ta ce"Daman ita ce?


murmushi Momy tayi Kadan Dan ta Gane nasren CE belah ta yarda da ita a park suna Karasowa Momy Ta nunawa Belah nasren ta na runtse Mata Ido ta baya ta ce.

"Baby na Suprised"Tana Jujjuyawa cikin gauranci ta ce "mah Ta Kan Ni"Yar Dariya mom tayi Tana bude Mata Ido ta ce

 "See ur mom"Kamar Wace Babba Haka belah Ta zaro Ido Zillo ta dingayi ta na Mika hannu alamun Momy Ta sauketa ta na sauketa dukul-dukul ta ruga da gudu wajen Nasren dake zaune k'asa ta Rungumeta.

 "itama nasren din Rungumeta tayi ta na Jin wani farinciki na Musamman na ratsa ta "Mom my"Belah ta Fada tana kara rungumeta Kafin kuka ta Fara kuka ta na dukanta Irin mutum yayima Laifi"Samun kanta nasren tayi ta Lallashinta Tana Bata Hakuri".

ba Momy ba harta Ni Miss hajo Saida suka burgeni Momy ko a boye ta goge hawayen da suka zubo Mata Laure ko mamaki ya Gama cika ta Har murza Ido take waiko mafarkine take ,

Tana Gama Lallashinta sai Kuma.ta lafe jikinta Kamar wata mage ta na Wasa da gaban hijab dinta wata nauyayyar ajiyar zuciya Momy ta sauke ta ce.

 "Alhamdulillah Allah mun gode maka"Kafin ta kalli Mama Laure ta ce "Kinga Abun al'ajabi ko"Laure na washe Baki ta ce 

"Wlhko Hajiya ai mamaki yasa na kasa magana"murmushi Momy tayi ta ce

 "Dole Kam Yanzu Zata zauna a Nan na Dauketa ba matsayin Mai raino kawai ba Zan dauketa matsayin y'a Zata dinga Zuwa Tun 7 Tana tafiya 6 sannan driver zai dinga Kaita ya na daukota na k'ara Miki 50k Akan 200k din da nace Zan Baki saboda Gaskiya kin kawomun Abun al'ajabi da mamaki gidan Nan da dole Nima na kyautata Miki ki jira Ina zuwa".


ta fada ta namikewa godiya wajen mama Laure kamar tayi Mata sujjada Kira ta ke"Allah Mai iko Ashe Arziki na sauke gida na bansani ba,nasmen Allah Miki Albarka keda Uwarki Kun Zama silar arzikina Ko dubu Dari ban Tab'a rikewa ba Amma yau nice da Kudi har dubu Dari Biyu da hamsin sai kukamn farinciki ta na Daman ance da d'a da dukiya ba a Musu mugunta,Ba a wulakanta d'an Adam bakasan amfanin da zai maka ba


 ,"haka tayi ta zantuttukanta barkatai nasren ko hankakinta Duk ya na Kan belah dake jikinta wani Irin yanayi takeji sabo a jikinta Tun haduwarta Da yarinya Allah ya Dora Mata Tsananin kaunarta Ta Musamman da a tunaninta burgeta ne yarinyar tayi sai Daga baya Ta fahimci ba burgewa bace kawai Tsananin kauna ce Allah ya dasa Mata Tata,Jin yarinyar take tamkar wani Bangare na jikinta ,jinta take tamkar ita ta Haife ta Dukda Batasan Ya zafin Soyayyar uwa akan d'a take ba Amma ta tabbatar Ko d'an da ta Haifa bazai wuce Irin Soyayyar wannan ba,saitaji ko ba sisi ta na iya Aikin Nan Musamman da Akace yarinyar marainiya CE saitaga ta Kara Bata tausayi ,to Amma meyasa take kirana da Momy tun farkon haduwarmu?me yasa Taki yarda da kowa Ni ta yarda Dani?Bata da Mai Bata Amsa Haka Momy ta fito rike da karamar jaka ta mikawa Mama Laure ta ce.


 "Ga tukuicinki"murna da godiya Har baka wajen mama Laure hada kukan farinciki Saida Momy ta ce ya Isa Haka Kafin tayi shiru ta na ta zabga godiya   driver Momy ta Kira ya mayar da mama Laure Dan ya Gano gidan Saida Ta kara yiwa nasren nasiha Akan ta rike Amana Kafin ta Tafi tanajinta ta zama wata billonia.

"Gyada Mata Kai nasren tayi ta na yaba Kirkin hajiyar Nuna Mata dakin tayi da Komai Kafin Ta nuna Mata Dakin Belah da kayanta Da Abincinta Yanda ake hadawa da Komai Har Lokacin belah na jikinta Taki Yarda ko Kadan ta saketa Koda aka Kira magrib Momy ta Mika hannu zata amshi Belah ta makale.

 "to Momy na Daina yi Hakuri"murmushi Momy ta sakar Mata ta ce "zanje nayi salla za a kawo Maki Abinci Da Isha'i sai driver ya mayar Dake Gida ko"?

 "Lokacin da nasren ta fito itama Ta bita Wai alwalar zatayi ganin zatayi Wasan ruwa ya sanyata shshafa Mata suka Fito duk yanda taga nasren tayi itama saitace saitayi wajen sallahma Dole nasren ta nada Mata gyale shima dakyal ta yarda ita Irin nata hijab din take so suna Gama sallar Mai Aiki ta shigo da Abinci Nasren na juyawa Sukayi Ido hud'u da Jamila .


Kadan ya Hana Bata Zubar da abincin ba Da gudu ta ruga ta na wayyo hajiya fatalwa Momy dake Fitowa ta Daka Mata tsawa Cikin bacin Rai ta ce.

"Kanwar uwarkice Kikaga tayi fatalwar Ta dawo Miki"ta na rurrudewa ta ce "Hajiya wlh Hajiya Babba nagani(Nabila?)Tsaki Momy ta ja ta ce

 "Nufinki nabilar ce fatalwa ko Dan Ubanki?Ta na girgiza Kai ta ce "Dan Allah kiyi Hakuri hajiya wlh ba haka nake Nufi ba nagantane shiyasa"Tana Jan kunnanta ta ce .

Bayan fitar Jamila nasren kanta dukya kulle Hakanan dai Ta chuchusa abincim belah na Mata gauranci itama ta Bata Abincinta Dukda Ta Bata natan Taki ci.

badan ran nasren yaso ba tabar belah ta Tafi ji take kamar ta koma ta dauketa Momy ko Gagara Lallashin Belah tayi bata taba botsare Mata irin haka ba ba Arziki ta Goyata Tana jijjigata a half wahalallen bacci ya dauketa Sai ajiyar zuxiya take saukewa.

Momy batace mishi kala ba Haka Bata dauki Belah da ta rude ta na tsala kuka ba Shi da Kanshi ya dauketa Yana Harararta ya ce"Bazakiyi shiru ba Saina zaneki?KE da bakya Taya Ubanki Yak'i ta gujemu,shine kike li'ke Mata Dan Baki Da Zuciya ta na Nuna Ba ma ta sanni ba shine Baki tayani Zuciya ba?


Dariya taso kubcewa Momy ta Tashi a ranta ta ce"Allah Mai iko Wai Belah yakewa Huduba,Tarasa ta Yanda zata fahimtar da Aliyu'ba Nabila bace ya fahimta".

A Gida Nasren bata da walwala Wanda mama Saida ta tambayeta lafiya Ta Ce bakomai Koda ta koma gidan Duk Yanda taso Ta Dauki Belah Abun gagara yayi Dan Aliyu' ya kanannade y'arsa Ranar Dakinsa ta kwana Nasren har kuka tayi Harga Allah tanason Belah Gashi Mahaifinta na son Raba su Ita batasan metayiwa Mutumin Nan ba ya tsaneta .

Yau Kawu Umar yazowa da Aliyu' maganar yaje Yaga Fadila Su daidaita kansu Dan bayason. Auren ya wuce wata Biyu"Cewa yayi ita tazo ta ganshi bai Jin Dadi (Lallai Aliyu)Shiko Kawu da lallabashi suke cewa yayi to shikenan gobe zatazo tayi Musu kwNa Biyu ma.

"yau su Fadila NE a gidan namu"Ta na karasowa Ta ce"kwana Biyu nazo yi Muku Momy Ina yini".

 Amma me Baisan mutuniyar Bata da dogon bacci ba ba Shiri ta tashin firgice Sosai ta tsorata ganin Aliyu' Yana kokarin yace Mata Riga Ihu ta kwalla da karfi ta ce"wayyo Allah na Shiga Ukku momy"Yana rufe Mata Baki ya ce"Wai baki da Hankali ne kike kurma ihu Cikin daren Nan ,da Allah Ni ba wani Abu ya kawoni ba Akwai abunda nake son dubawa ".


"ya Karasa fada ya na fisgota Tare da Kara juyar da ita Bayanta Yake son Gani wani ihun ta Kuma kurmawa Momy Daman ihunta na farko ya tadota ba Shiri ta karaso Hankalinta tashe Ta kunna hasken taga suna kiciciniya da Aliyu' , innalillahi wa inna ilaihiraji'un Aliyu' me nake Gani Haka Wai baka Da Hankali kome?

"Sakarta nace ko ranka yayi mugun baci na Shiga ukku ni zainabu "Jin muryar momy ya sanyashi juyowa idanunshi harsun chanza kala ya ce"Momy kije ba ruwanki Akwai abunda nakeson dubawa Ki fita please *MATA TA CE* Ba wani Abu bane Dan kin ganmu tare"tsawa momy  ta Daka Masa ta ce "ta gidan ubanwa ta Zama matarka Aliyu'ko Dan Naga na saka maka Ido duk shirman Nan da kake Ina kyaleka wuce kaban wuri ko ranka yayi mugun baci".


wani Irin Kara yayi ya na Sauka Ba Shiri Wurin Da yake kallo momy ta kalla Ta na zaro Ido Ganin Katuwar kunama Tana Shirin Cizo kusa Da Nasren da Idonta KE rumtse ta rik'e hannayenta"Matsa Daga nan Aliyu' Nasren Tashi kunama"Tuni Aliyu' ya koma kusa da momy ya na cewa"Ki matsa soulmate karta cijeki".


"Daidai Lokacin Da Nasren ta bude Idonta  ta na hucin tashin Hankali Da gudu ta ruga ta na boyewa Bayan mommy ta na kuka ta ce"Dan Allah Mommy ki maidani Gida,na hakura da rainon wlh na dinga zuwa Ina ganin belahr idan Kun Yarda Amma Dan Allah momy ki maidani Gida".


"Lallashinta mommy ta dinga yi ta ce"Kuyi hakuri Yanzu dare ne Kafin ta harari Aliyu' ta ce"Hankalinka ya kwanta Aliyu' Nasren zata bar maka gidan Nan saika zubda ruwa a kasa ka sha" 


Tana Gama Fadar haka Ta ja Hannun Nasren Kafin ta ce"ga y'arka chan Saika Dauketa ku tafi tunda duk halarcin da Yarinyar Nan Tayi Mana baka gani ba "A dakin ta Momy ta zaunar da Nasren ta ce.

"Okay koma ki kwanta nasren duk wasu hujjoji da nake nema sun kusa Haduwa"a Zuciyar momy kara jinjina abun take Yaudai sai bayyanuwa Abubuwa suke Mata a saukake Kwaffin tagwaitakar su Da Nabila ne Yau Ma Ta gwada Dan Allah yakara gwada mata ishara ,Daman Nabila Tana Kwaffi ai Na mugunta ma.Ko a Makaranta dalilin da yasa aka fiya shakkunta ,Anan ma Tasha gwada Musu Musamman akan Aliyu'.

Washegari da Zazzabin gaske ya tashi Saida momy ta basa magani tare da kiran Dr Yayi Masa allura Mai hade da ta bacci tukwana Hankalinta ya Dan Kwanta ganin bacci ya dauke sa.

Zaune ta Samu nasreen a palour tayi shiru duk surutun da belah ke mata tamak'i kulata hankalinta duk baya wajen"Bayan momy ta zauna ta ce"Ina Kara Baki hakuri nasren Akan Abunda Ya Faru Jiya,Ina son kiyi Hakuri kiyiwa Aliyu' uzuri,karki dauki abunda ya Faru Jiya da wata manufa,Nasan halin Aliyu' ba haka yake ba kawai dai abubuwane sukazo da rud'u Amma komai ya kusa zuwa karshe kiyi Hakuri"kanta kasa Tana Wasa da yatsun Hannunta' ta ce.

 "Bakomai momy"Kince kina son ki koma Gida ko Nasren bazan takuraki ba,idan kinngaji da Zama damu Bazamu takuraki ba tunda kina da yancin kanki"Kallon belah tayi tuni Zuciyarta ta karye harga Allah batajin zata iya rabuwa Da Belah Tana Hawaye ta ce"Kiyi hakuri momy jiyanma tsoro ne yasa na Fadi haka ,Amma yanda na shaku da Belah banajin Zan iya Rabuwa da ita"Nauyayyar ajiyar zuciya momy ta sauke ta ce.


"ina zuwa"Sama taje Batayi 10 minutes ba ta dawo bandir din 1k biyu a Hannunta' Ta na Zama ta ce"zoki Amsa Nasren"Zuwa tayi ta Mika Mata da mamaki ta Amsa ta ce"na meye mommy?Albashinki ne Nasren Case dinnan ya Hana ban Baki ba kiyi Hakuri"Cikin sauri Nasren ta Ajewa momy a gefenta ta Ce"


"Kiyi hakuri momy Amma bazan iya amsar ko sisi a saboda rainon Nabila Ba"Bafa saboda rainon na Baki ba nasren kyautace nayi maki Dan Allah ki Amsa zanji Dadi ba kyau maida hannun kyauta baya"Kara girgiza Kai tayi ta ce"a a a mommy nagode Dan Allah Kiyi hakuri Amma bazan iya amsa ba" ba "Duk yanda Momy tayi ta Amsa kiyawa tayi karshe ta kyaleta a ranta ko ba k'aramun Dadi Taji ba tabbas Tasan Nabila Tayi uwa d'aya d'aya tayi Rashin MAHAIFIYA Amma Bata rasa gatan uwa ba ta tabbatar da Nasren da Zuciya Daya take son belah tun Yanzu Tana Mata irin wannan son inaga Kuma ta gano y'ar Yar uwarta ce.

"ta inda muka banbance Nabila.ce hasana ma'ana ta Fara zuwa Shine wani Dambareren Bak'in Abu a kasar bayanta kamar two colour haka ta haka ne muke banbancesu sai kuma da suka Fara dariya Muka lura Nasren nada dimples sab'anin Nabila

 ,Sosai nashiga farinciki kusan kullum Saina fita na jibgo sayayyar tsaraba Wani Karin har dariya yakemun Wai Ina zumudin da yawa Saidai nayi murmushi Ranar wata Talata Da bazan taba mantawa ba Tunda Zan fita Sayayya Jikinsa Yayi sanyi Yana kallona ya ce"Khalbi Karki fita yau Banason fitar Nan(Kasancewar haka yake kirana dashi kunsan Larabawa Da iya Soyayya Da kalamai)
