"Yau kuma Kaga rigimar sukeji Kuma bazan iya fita Dasu duka ba Dama duka sun zauna Sainaje nadawo Hankali kwance"jikinshi duk a sanyaye ya ce"bani Nabilar Muga ,Ina Mika Masa Ita Ta Makale abunda Bata Tabamunba Saina lura da kallon da takemun na Yauma ya banbanta da kullum duk Munyi mamaki Ga Kuma Nasren dake tsala Kuka Duk Nima sainaji wani iri zaunawa nayi Duka biyun suna jikina inda na taba Jikin Nabila naji lafiya qlau Addua na Tofa Mata a goshi Kafin Nashiga Lallashinta Motsi kadan nace ko Baki lafiya dukda Bata magana Bayan na shayar da ita na Goyata Cikin kankanin lokaci bacci ya dauketa Ina sauke ajiyar zuciya Na Sauke ta a Hankali na dorata Hannun babanta yana Kwantar da ita Kafin na dauki Nasren da yau takejin rigima Na Goyata Saida muka sake Sallama Yana shaidamun Dan Allah Karna Jima Kafin na tafi Ina waigen Nabila haka kawai Lokacin naji wani Irin barinta da zanyi dukda Na Saba Tafiya babu ita Hakan yasa duk nayi sanyi da fitar Kai tsaye kasuwa na Nufa Daman Yau Takalma Ne nakeson saya na tsaraba Saidai Ranar tsautsayi ya gitta dani .

Askarawa suka dira kasuwar Kamen Takaru Mutane sai guduwa ake ana kamosu Kamar wasu dabbobi a Ranar ne aka kamani tare da Nasren Nasha magiya Da Rokonsu taimakamun na baro y'ata a Gida ,Amma kamarma Kara zuga su nake Lurar da nayi kwata-kwata Basu San Ace Musu Dan Allah ba satina Biyu a sijin ga ba waya gareniba Allah ya taimakenima Da kudi na A Jikina Na sayayyar da banyiba Wanda suka Kusan haura dubu Dari tun lokacinma a Lokacin aka maido da Kowa kasarsa Nasha Kuka Da takaicin fitowata ga tunanin y'ata da mijina da na baro da ya addabeni nanfa na Shiga wani halin Rayuwar komawata Da Nasren na Zama kamar mujiya cikin Al'umma kowa Kira ake nadawo da Shegiyar d'iyar Larabawa naje yawon duniya anyimun Ciki,maganganu Dai bakyan ji.
 Musamman Yan uwa na Dan Lokacin Matan baba Ashe sun Mutu a hanyarsu Ta zuwa wajen malamansu sukayi accident Abun ba Kyan  gani Ciki hada babbar yayarmu ,fir Yan uwana sukaki su bani Koda daki a gidan dukda Akwai gadona a gidan cin mutuncin da korar Kare sukamin Ina Kuka Ina komai Na Shiga gidan makwabciyata da tamun hanyar Saudia Nan nake samun Labarin mijinta Ya Mutu yayarta Tayi' Mata Aiki ko wata Guda ba Ayi ba ta koma Saudia Aikatau Rana zafi inuwa Kuna haka nayi ta gara-ramba Allah ya taimakeni na chanza Kud'in hannuna dubu dari da saba'in da su Na Kama Hayar gidan Dubu talatin a shekara Inda Na Biya Na shekara Biyu Sauran na Dan saya Mana abubuwan Bukata Tare da Yan Kayan sawa Mararsa tsada Dan da Kayan jikinmu kadai mukazo Kafin na sayi Injin markade da Kayan miya tunda na lura unguwar Akwai cikowar mutane Sosai Nasha Wahala Kafin Nasren ta saba da Rayuwar kazaure duk dare saita dinga mun Kuka Lurar da nayi zafi ne bataso saidai na watsa mata ruwa in zauna inyi ta mata Fifita har tayi' bacci Allahsrk yarinyar data Saba kwana a lumtsumemen gado cikin ac Da kamshi yau itace KE kwanan Bargo.


Cikin ikon  Allah Ina samun ciniki dashi na dinga rufawa kanmu asiri har Nasren tayi karatu Amma har kullum da Y'ata da mijina nake kwana a Raina duk wata hanya da zanbi ganin An sadani Dasu babu Gashi babu ta yanda za ayi na koma Saudia Ina ta abunda zamuci ma a takaice dai wannan shine tarihin Rayuwata da ta Nasren Babban burina Bai wuce ganin Y'ata Nabila da mijina ba a Halin Yanzu Harna cire ran Haduwa Dasu".

"ta Karasa Fada cikin Kuka Gaba Daya kowa Saida yayi Kuka Musamman Momy Dan tunda ta Fara labarin take Kuka ,Allah Mai iko duk dukiyar Yaya Y'arsa da matarsa suka Sha wannan wahalar Ashe Da gaske Nasren twins din Nabila ce ,ya mama zataji ta Samu labarin mutuwar Nabila,Kuka kawai Momy take Haka nasren da lokaci Guda tasa ran haduwarta da Yar uwarta dakyal suka tsagaita kukan mama Laure ta ce"

"murmushi Momy ta Kuma Ta na Dafa Kafadar Mama ta CE"Alkawari ne na Daukar Miki ki zauna cikin Shirin Tarbar mijina Kafin ta kallii Nasren ta ce "ki shirya Haduwa da Mahaifinki Nasren Kafin ta Mike tanajin jiri na neman debarta Ta Kama Hannun Nasren ta ce Kuzo Muje "Ba Musu suka bita kowa ya Daskare Yana mamakin maganar Momy har suka Isa Gida Nasren na Hawaye Momy Batayi kokarin hanata ba Dan itama so take ta Samu Guri tai Kukan son ranta.

 ,haka Yar uwarta Bata Mutu ba Nabila ta tafi wata Nabilar ta dawo"To Amma momy kina ganin Uncle zaizo k'asar Nan kuwa alhalin Bai taba zuwa ba duk shekarun ki a Nan"Tana Jan numfashi ta ce"zaizo inshallah idan muka hada plane Daga gobe zaka kirasa Ka shaida Masa Banda Lafiya Ba Yanda nake ,A haka Har mu tada Masa Hankali Da kanshi Zaitaho"Momy kina ga wannan shawarar tayi"zatayi ma Inshallah"Yana sauke ajiyar zuciya ya ce"Inshallah Haka za ayi"a ranshi Yana Kara jinjinawa Kenan Shiyasa Belah ta yarda da Nasren d'in Kanwar Mamanta ce shiyasa Itama Nasren din ta so Belah Ashe ba banza baitama Momy Da wannan tunaninka ta dingayi ta na Jinjina hikimar Allah Gashi ta Silar Belah komai zai warware.

 "Alhamdulillahi Allah na gode maka Daka nunamun wannan Rana Da gaske Kenan yau ni ce na hadu da mahaifina Bayan gori Da tozarcin da Nasha wajen Mutane na bani da uba Ashe Nima Ina dashi"ta Karasa Fada ta na Hawaye karasawa wajen mama tayi itama hawayen farincikin ne take ji take kamar ta rungumosu Ko ta tabbatar da gaskene Kamo Hannunsa tayi da Idonshi har Lokacin a kanta ta ce "Da Gaske Kaine "Yana sakar Mata murmushi Hawaye Nabin gefen fuskarsa ya ce"Da gaske nine Asma'u Allah Ya kamata kiyiwa godiya ,na nemeki keda Nasren Harna cire ran sake haduwa Daku a Duniya Sai Gashi yau Allah ya kadarta ta yanda ban Tab'a tsammani ba Ina Kika shige tsawon Lokacin Nan Asma'u"Mama na Hawaye ta ce"Wannan Bayanin sai mun ,zauna Ina y'ata Nabila Yanzu burina Bai wuce Ganinta ba Tunda Allah ya bayyanamun Kai Allah nagode maka Ta na Kara rik'e Hannunsa Sosai ta ce "Ina Nabila Take.....✍️

Gyaran Jiki Da Aka Fara Mata wata balarabiya Aka dauko Musamman Gaba Daya ukkun take Musu gyara momy,mama,Nasren kunsan larabawa Da gyara Tuni Fatarsu ta murje sun Kara wani sihirtaccen kyau Ga kamshi Tako Ina,Musamman Nasren gyara ya hadu da yarinta Sati guda kenan saitaji Duk kewar Aliyun ta Fara addabarta.


Bangaren Aliyu komawarsa rigima Yasha Dasu Kawu Wai Shikenan ya fifita Dangin uwarsa a kansu Harsu Kara D'aura Masa wani Auren waiwani Yan biyun Nabila ,Ina suka San Nabila nada wata Yan Biyu to dole ko me yake ya hada Har Fadila Ya aura nanfa Aliyu ya gwada Musu kalar nashi taurin Kai ba Wanda ya Isa Ya aura Masa ita cikinsu Tunda Shi ba Yaro bane ,Kuma Basu suke da Alhakin wannan Ba tas yayi Musu kafin suyi zuru-zuru su koma lallami Amma fir yakiya Karshe Dai Saida Ya hadasu da manya-manyan kudade Kafin ya samu su sama Masa lafiya.


Yanzu gaba Daya tunaninsa Naga Sabuwar Amaryarsa Yanzu ma ya na kwance ya na tunaninta Soulmate dinsa Ta Fado Masa Arai A zuciyarsa ya ce"Allah ya jikanki soulmate yasa kina cikin Rahama ,Allah hadamu a aljanna FIDDAUSI kiyi hakurin Auren Yar uwarki Da Nayi Haka Allah ya rubuta Mana Nasan Kema zakiyi farinciki da haka,Aliyunki ya samu madadinki,Y'arki Nabila ma Ta samu madadinki wacce Take yi Mata soyayyar uwa da y'a


Satin Aliyu Biyu ya Dawo wanda Ganin sabren din yayi ta Dawo Masa sabuwa ta Kara Masa kyau da murjewa Dukda kunyarsa da kawaicin da take Bai hanata nuna Masa farincikin dawowarsa ba.


Washegari mommy ta tare Gidan Sabon Mijinta Ita kadaice gidan sai ma'aikata Dan dayar matar tasa Ba Gida Daya Ya hada su ba.
Nanfa Abba ya ce a Fara shirye-shiryen Tarewar Nasren Da Aliyu Tunda an Riga an D'aura Auren kowa ya tare Gidan sa farinciki wajen Aliyu kamar Yayi tsalle Nanfa Nasren Hankalinta ya tashi ita harga Allah tazata daura Auren Akayi Tarewarma har saita Kammala Makarantarta da Aka sama Mata Addmission ,Saida Abba yabar wajen Kafin tasa Kuka ta na shigewa Jikin mama ta ce.


"Dan Allah Mama kiyi Masa magana Ya Bari Saina Gama Makaranta Ni banason tarewar Yanzu"Mama na Lallashinta ta ce"Haba Nasren Mahaifinki Zaki bujirewa ko me?kanwarsa ma Yake Bata umarni Bata tsallakewa bare ke y'arsa ko so kike a ce ban Baki tarbiya me kyau ba"Saurin girgiza kai Tayi ta ce "A a a mama "To Indai Kinason farincikina karki Tada Maganar Nan kome Mahaifinki Ya Fada ki amince Kai tsaye kinsani ai bazai chutar dake ba "Kara gyadawa mama Kai Tayi"Allah Miki Albarka Yar kirki Kije kar Y'ar Taki ta tashi Saida safe"To mama"ta Fada ta na Mikewa Yayinda Mama Itama Ta Mike.


Sati na zagayowa Aka Shirya gagarumin biki na Musamman bikin da ya hada manya-manyan kasar Nan Domin halarta Lunching bikin Y'ar Shahararren hamshakin Mai arzikin Nan Watau (Anan)Amarya da ango sun Sha Kyau Kamaar Ka sace su Ka ruga ,Uncle ma Uban amarya ya Sha Kyau Na musamman kunsan dai yanda Larabawa suke haka momy Mai gayya Mai Aiki haka mama Gaba daya Daka kallesu kasan kowanne ya jiku Da naira Mama ma Tayi kalar nata Kyan Na musamman.

 Liyafar data had'a shuwagabannin kasashe Sama Da Goma ,Banda manya manyan Sarakuna ,Da malumma Anyi liyafa Irin ta Addinin Musulunci Manyan Malamai Sun gabatar da wa'azuzzuka kala kala Anci ansha Anyi nishadi Belah ma Tasha kyau Irin rigar da Nasren tasa Itama Irinta aka saya mata duk inda ka hangosu saika sake kallonsu camerori Iri-iri a kansu ba a magana Daga Karshe aka Watse Taro da Addua Bayan an koma Gida An sake shirya amarya Shirin Musamman aka wuce Da ita Sabon gidan mijinta da shima ya gaji Da Haduwa dake wata unguwar da ban fadar haduwar gidan ma da furnitures din da aka zuba b'ata Baki ne kunsandai tilor y'ar Anan Daya Tak data rage Masa Dole Komai ya banbanta da yanda duk za a kwatanta bazai kwatantu ba.


 Sosai Nasren din Tasha Kuka Bayan Manya sunyi Mata Nasiha zasu juya da Belah Nanfa Tace Bata San zancen ba ta makalkale Belah Itama belahr tasa Kuka ta na makalkaleta wata daga cikinsu ta Ce.


"Hajiya Zainab fa Tace Kar a barota kome zatayi a tafar Mata da ita"Nasren na Kuka ta ce "Nidai ba inda zata"Matar kawar momy  itama a Nja take ta ce"Allah Mai iko" to Bari na kirata kiji da kunnanki"Bayan ta Kira momy ta karawa Nasren wayar ta ce"Ki Bari a taho da ita Nasren gobe Tunda safe zansa a kawo Miki ita'"Tana Kuka ta ce


"nidai a a momy"amsar wayar kawar momy Tayi mommy tace kawai su tafi"Suna Tafiya Nasren ta sauke Ajiyar Zuciya Tana cigaba Da Hawaye Belah Dake kukan Itama Ta Fara share Mata Hawaye ta na cewa "Shorly(sorry)Momy kiy hakuli(kiyi hakurin)Murmushi Nasren ta sakar Mata Tana Gyada Mata Kai Kafin ta dorata a Cinyarta Ta ce.


"na hakura Kema yi shiru"      Daidai shigowar Aliyu Yasha babbar Riga Ubansu Sai baza kamshi Yake yasha Kyau na Musamman

Rike da manya-manyan Ledoji ya shigo Ga mamaki ya kalli Belah Dake jikinta Tana shshekaka jiki sanyaye ya karaso ya na aje ledar tare da Zama Gefenta Yana kallonta Yanda kanta ke Kasa Taki yarda su hada Ido ya kalli Belah ya ce

"Kukanme kike Daughter"Tana Kallonshi ta nuna Mai Nasren Ta ce"Mo my  ch'es crying"(she's crying)murmushi yayi ya na kallonsu Nasren Sosai Tayi Masa kyau cikin farar gown dinkin yayi das a jikinta nadin head Dinta yayi mugun kyau rabin fuskar nata a rufe Nauyayyar Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke jikinsa ne yayi sanyi tunowa da Ranar aurenshi da Belah baisan sanda wasu hawayen masu dumi suka zubo Masa ba yayi sauri ya goge Yana Kiran sunanta a sanyaye ,bata Dago ba Dukda Tajisa Numfashi yaja ya na kallon Belah Yana langwabewa ya ce

"kinji ta kyaleni ko?Kamar wata babba ta daddabar Nasren ta ce"mo my"Itama kyaleta Tayi Dan Allah-Allah take Ya fita"Belah na Shagwabe fuska ta ce"Dady Ba ta hakuli (hakuri)"murmushi yayi ya ce"To my Belah Jeki Palour Daukomun jaka ta Kan sopa kinji"da sauri ta Gyada Masa Kai ta na sauka daga gadon ta ruga ,Ajiyar Zuciya ya sauke ya na Zama gefenta Fara'a dauke da fuskarsa ya yaye lullubin rumtse idonta Tayi da karfi Abun dariya ma ya basa tabbas Kunya ado ce ga y'a mace Shifa harga Allah kunyar Belah Burgesa Take..

 "Yau Kuma tsoro na kike Kanwata"Sunan da ya kirata dashi ne ya sanyata Dagowa Harga Allah Sunan ya Mata Dadi Amma haka Nan taji kishi Tana narai-narai da Ido ta ce"Daman ai nasan baka Sona saboda Belah ka aure Ni ,Watau ita ce kawai matar ka Ni Kuma Kanwarka ".

ta Karasa Fada Tana Hawaye "Yana rungomota kadan ta gefensa Yaso yayi dariya Yana Daddaba bayanta ya CE"Waya Fada Maki Bana sonki my Nasren Ina sonki tun Lokacin Da na Fahimci Ke ba Nabila Bace,Bana son Koda Wasa a zamanmu Dake kiyi kishin Yar uwarki kinji?Ina son mu Gina Sabuwar rayuwa Mai Cike da farinciki,Nishad'i,Da Jin dad'i,nasan ko Baki fad'a ba kina Sona kamar yanda Nima nake kaunarki Ina fatan Zaki rikeni da Amana ,mu rikewa juna Amana da sirri sannan muyi hakuri da juna,ta bangaren belah nasan bani da matsala da wannan na tabbata abunda Zaki Mata ko mahaifiyarta Albarka Allah yayi Miki Albarka Nasren ya Tabbatar da Alkhairi a cikin Rayuwar Aurenmu ya Kauda fitina ki Daina kishin Yar uwarki kinji"

Ita kanta sai sannnan taji wani Irin Maganar Tata Tana Hawaye ta ce"Bazan sake ba Yaya Amma Dan Allah Ina neman Alfarmar idan Belah Ta girma Kar Wanda ya sanar mata banice Mamanta ba muddin Hakan ta faru zanfi kowa farinciki Dan Allah "yana Murmushi ya ce"Inshallah Amaryata da tazomun da Y'ar leelen y'arta"rufe fuska Tayi tana Murmushi.

BELAH na shigowa ta nufi wajensa tana fada Masa da gauranci bataga jakar ba ya ce"To shikenan"Umartar Nasren da Tayo alwala yayi tare sukayo har Belah uwar iyayi shi daman da alwalarsa ya shigo

 Sabuwar hijab Dinta har kasa ya dauko Mata ta Sanya itama ya Sanyawa belah tata Kafin ya jasu Salla Raka'a biyu suna gamawa ya Jiyo ya dafa goshinta Yana karanto adduo'i ya Jima yanayi kamar wani Babban Sheikh Saida ya Gama Kafin su shafa Baki Daya Plate ya Mike yaje ya dauko Yana dawowa ya iske Tana cirema Belah hijab din.

 Dankara-dankaran kaji Guda Biyu ya Juye a plate din Bayan ya tsiyaya Fresh milk a cup Duk Yanda yayi kokarin ya Bata taci kiyawa Tayi ta Ce batajin yunwa shima Gagara Ci yayi ga mamakinshi i Belah da son nama itama cewa Tayi ta Koshi Yayi yayi Nasren taci takiya Karshe dakyal ya samu ta Dan Sha fresh milk din ga mamakinshi Itama belah saida taga mom Dinta Tasha ta Sha.

 murmushi yayi ya girgiza Kai ya ce"Watau had'emun Kai zaku dingayi ko,to ai shikenan Nima Zan rama'"Kafin ya aje Musu a saman center table Bayan ya saka Madarar da lemukan a fridge Kallon Nasren da duk ta d'are yayi ya ce 

"To Saida safe ko Amaryata "Kanta kasa Ta Gyada Masa Kai"Murmushi yayi ya na girgiza Kai Kafin ya bawa Belah peck a goshi Yana shammatarta itama ya bata a goshinta Kafin ya ce"Good Night Habibty"Saida numfashin Nasren ya kusa daukewa Kafin Ya Dawo Harga Allah ya shammaceta Tana Jin fitarsa ta sauke Ajiyar Zuciya Ta na Dago Kai Belah ko tsalle Tayi ta na Dariya ta ce.

"mo my mun mushyy wayu ko(mun mushi wayau ko)Yar Dariya Nasren tayi tana gyada Mata Kai ta Mike tana rufo k'ofar ta Dawo Naman ta dauko suka baje gado ita da belah Dake dariyar sunyiwa Dady wayau zasu cinyee su barsa nasren data biyeta har kashewa suke saida suka Koshi suka bi da fresh milk ruwa ta watsawa Belah ta sa mata Kayan bacci itama ta saka Kafin subi lafiyar gado bacci Mai Dadi na daukarsu.

Washegari misalin takwas sun shirya Sosai Sukayi kyau ita da yarta gwanin sha'awa ango ma Aliyu ya sha gayu tare sukayi breakfast da abincin da aka kawo Musu daga gidan momy ,Ranar da yamma aliyu ya bawa nasren sabuwar waya Mai shegen kyau da tsada sosai nasren tayi matukar farinciki karon farko ta fara rik'e waya tun Ranar ya fara koya mata Abubuwa saboda yanzu ta saki jiki dashi Sosai.

Sati Biyu momy tayi ta na aiko Musu da girki,safe,Rana,dare,a satin Nasren ta ce momy tabar wahalar da kanta zata dingayi Ranar da Aliyu yaci girkin Nasren yayi Santi ba kadan ba ya kara jinjinawa Nasren din Tako Ina ,tabbas wani Rashin gatan gata ne ya tabbatar da Nasren gidan Uncle ta tashi ko ruwan tea baxata taba iya dafawa ba kamar Nabila da ko gas Bata taba kunnawa ba da Sunan girki bama Tasan ya akeyinsa ba Har tabar duniya taci ta huta kawai ta sani ,.

Yau Mama Ta Kira Aliyu Bayan sun gaisa dukda Yana Dan Jin nauyinta itako ta saki jiki dashi sai Hira suke Inda daga Karshe ta ce "Gobe Dan Allah ka kawo Mana Belah Ta Mana kwana Biyu ,kasan Abban naku da Yara Wai yayi kewarta"to mama inshallah goben za a kawota "Saida suka Taba Hira Kafin suyi Sallama Yana sauke Ajiyar Zuciya ,.

Washegari Koda zai tafi Da Belah boyewa Nasren yayi ya ce "ice cream zai sayo Mata ,Shagwaba ta dinga Masa kamar zatayi kuka Wai ita tsoro take ga magrib Tayi Bata iya Zama gidan,dakyal ya lallasheta yace Minti biyu zasuyi su dawo wannan Karin Belah da Yake yawo ne itace hadawa Nasren gwalo,harararta Tayi ta CE "Ni ko?zamu hade ai"Dariya ta dinga mata shikuma Yana boye tasa dan karta tubure haka dai ya samu ya lallasheta Tana cewa ta Basu Minti goma ,.
Saida ya biya ya sayawa Belah abubuwan makuleshe kafin su wuce mama na mitar me yasa Bai taho da kayanta ba ya ce mantawa yayi Dayake Ta Saba Da su mama Da Abban Yana wayau ya tafi bama ta sani ba.

Saida yabiya yayiwa Nasren sayayyar Itama Kayayyakin makuleshen kafin ya wuce gidan.

Lokacin Daya Isa ta na bedroom Hakan yasa ya Fara Shiga nashi bedroom din Bayan ya yo wanka Ya dau gayun farar shadda sai sheki take kunsan dai yanda Aliyu yake da daukar wanka.

A Hankali ya tura kofar bata jima da Fitowa daga wanka ba tana gaban mirror Tana shafe-shafen turarruka ,tsaye yayi daga bakin Kofa Yana kallonta batare da ta Jiyo ba ta Ce"My Angel Harkun Dawo kenan,Ina Tawa tsarabar"

"Ta na Goge Mata Hawaye ta kirkiri Murmushi ta ce "Yi shiru My Beloved daughter Ni da dadynki Bazamu Mutu ba "Daidai shigowar Mimah kyakyawar balarabiya itama dai sak Belah ce Mai shekara hud'u duk Yaran kaman Belah sukayo Dukansu Masha Allah Daka kallesu Kaga Larabawa sak Jikinta duk jambaki Rik'e da hotunan Tana rugowa ta Fada Jikin Nasren Tana nuna Mata wani hoton ta ce .

"momy Kalli KE da dady"Kallon hoton Nasren Tayi Nabila ce tare da Aliyu Lokacin sunje honey moon ta Rungumosa ta baya suna Dariya Duka yaran Kallon hoton suka dingayi Belah ce ta ce"Wow Momy kunyi kyau keda dady "Arif ne yace "momy ya Akayi dimples Dinki suka k'i Fitowa a hoton Bayan Kinyi Dariya Kuma"

Amshe hotunan Duka Nasren tayi ta na rufewa ta ce "Oya Muje A shirya Dady ya kusa dawowa"Belah na tsalle ta ce "yee dady Daman yace yau zai sayomun Fura da nono na sha ta Nigeria"Nan suka hau Murna Kafin ta Dauki Mimah Ta ce oya Kuje Arif saura ku b'ata Toilet din da ruwa kuga Yanda Zan zaneku"Arif na Dariya ya ce "sannu Aunty Babba Bayanma na fiki karfi Duka fa Shekara Biyu Da Rabi Abba yace kinban".

Tana Masa gwalo ta ce Aidai na baka Kuma Kayi laifi Kaga in ban zaneka ba "Yana Dariya ya ce Sorry my Aunty bazan sake ba "Su Biyu suka nufi bedroom dinsu Kafin ta kalli Nasren da duk Tayi sanyi ta ce "Momy Kema Kije Ki shirya Kar Dady ya dawo Bakiyi wanka ba

 "Murmushi Tayi ta ce"To"Suna shigewa Ciki Tayi kukan da Bata samu Tayi ba Harga Allah Ta Jima da mantawa da ba ita ta haifi Belah ba saiyau da Yaran suka tuna Mata dukda Bata tabajin ba ita ta haifeta Dan wlh Yanda takeson Belah ko yaran data Haifa Bataso Haka Tana tsananin kaunarta,Addua Tayi Allahsa Kar Belah ta taba Sanin Ba itace Ta haifeta ba Saikuma wata Zuciyar Tace wama zai Fada Mata Bayan Bama Zaman kasar muke ba sai shekara muke zuwa hutu.

 Kuma ,momy dai Bazata taba Fada Mata ba,haka Abba ,haka mama ,bare mama Laure,Dan su kadai suka San Haka Sai Dangin Babanshi Da Bama Zuwa wajensu suke ba Suma din Kuma Babu Mai Fada Ajiyar Zuciya ta sauke tanajin sassauci kafin ta Mike tsaye sai sannan na lura da k'aramun cikin dake jikinta da Bai wuce wata Biyar ba.
Kafin 8pm duka sun sake Sabon gayu ka kalli wannan family din dole su birgeka Duba Da Yanda suka taso cikin tsantsan hadin Kai ga Uwa uba kyau,gayu tsafta

 Nasren ma Tayi kyau Sosai cikin dinkin lace Dinta Suna Jin sallamar Aliyu Dukansu Yaran suka Mike Da gudu suna rungumesa Sosai Naga Aliyun ya Kara Zama wani cikakken hamshaki, Daya Bayan Daya ya dinga daukarsu har Belah Saida ya daukesu Nasren da ta Mike tana Murmushi ta ce.

"sannu da dawowa My Qalbi"Yana Murmushi Ya ware Mata hannaye "ya ce "Saura Ke Habibty"Dariya'Tayi Tana Makale kaf'ad'a ta ce "um um Muje dai Ka shirya Ka huta "Belah na Dariya ta ce "Dady kunyarmu Take Kuma ma mu ba kallonku zamuyi ba"Amir ma na Dariya ya ce

"Eh momy mu saima mu rimtse idonmu"Arif Kuma Ya na Dariya ya ce "Ni Saina tashi ma gaba D'aya"Nasren na Shagwabe fuska ta ce "Kaga ko zasu Fara Janmu Sarai kasan Yaran Nan da shegen zolaya Kuma kasan Yanda suka Sa Mana Ido"Yana Kama Hannunta' ya ce "To Muje rabu Dasu duk zamuyi Maganinsu Da Sabon Babynmu Yazo Zamu Daina kulasu "

Belah na Dariya ta ce "Dady sai Sabon Baby Yazo Meyasa Yanzu bazakuyi ba "Arif na Dariya ya ce"ai Dan bazasu iya ba shiyasa"Dariya kawai Sukayi suka nufi bedroom dinsu Amer ya ce "momy Kika Jima Bazamu rage Miki tsarabar da dady yayi Mana ba "ya fada da Karfi"Ku cinye Zan bata Mata wani Wanda yafima naku dad'i "Aliyu ya fada"Daidai shigarsu dakin "Nasren na Dariya ta ce "Allah Qalbi kaike biyewa Yaran nan"To ai in ba a biye Musu ba saisu ballowa Mutane ruwa "Y'ar Dariya Tayi ta ce"Kuma fa hakane"Kafin su Shiga babin Soyayya Yana Fada Mata kalamai masu Dadi Tana mayar Masa.

Saida suka kusa cinye duk abunda Ya saya Musu kafin Belah ta dauke ledar ta ce"kudai Baku da Kunya nufinku zamu cinye bamu ragewa momy ba Kunma Isa "Arif ya ce"Dady fa ya sayo mata wani natama cewa yayi sunfi Namu Dadi"Amir ya ce "to ai Kuma Bai Shiga Da leda ba"Mimah Dake jagular chocolate ta ce.

 "A mota ya bar Mata Dan Kal mu Gani"Amir yace "Lah eh wlh wayau suke mana saisunyi bacci suke Fitowa suci Abincinsu bakuga ba basuyin dinner tare Damu ba Rannanma Ina jinsu suna cin Nama a Palour Ranar na fito zansha ruwa"Belah ta ce "dole fa momy saitacii Kayan Nan ku ko Kunya ma bakuci Ni ko a school banama iya cin Abu ban ragewa momy ba Bayan ita kome taci saita bamu".

Amir ya ce "mu ai ba Sosai take bamu kayanta ba Jiya ma KE kad'ai ta bawa furar da aka Bata Wai Dan Bata Da yawa ai ta fima sonki "Arif na Dariya ya ce "Eh Mana Daman ai anfi son d'an farko inji Friends dina ya ce daga na Farko sai na Biyu ake so kunga Keenan daga Aunty Belah ,saini Momy Take so Sai Kai amir Mimah ce karshen ba a sonta Idan Kuma momy ta haifi babynta Shi zata fiso Akan Mimah Mimah ba a santa Lolo".

"ya fada ya na Mata gwalo take Mimah tasanya Kuka Tana Ihu Wai sunce ba asonta Belah na Dariya Ta rikota Tana Musu Dariya ta ce"Kaga ka Sanya ta kuka mugu "yi shiru kanwata Momy Duka Tama fi sonki Nima Kuma nafi sonki Kallansu duka bana sansu ke kadai nake so momyn Dady kinga saboda Dady yafi sonki Sunan momynshi ma yasa Maki Ko Rabu Dasu duk sai mun zanesu"sai sannan Tayi shiru Tana turo Baki ta harari su Arif ta ce "Kuma Duka Saina Sa momyn Dady ta Daina muku girki Mai Dadi".

"Belah na Dariya ta ce "Banda Ni ai ko mimahn Dady"Gyada Mata Kai Tayi ta ce "eh aunty Kuma ke.kadai nake so duk na Daina sonsu saisun Bani hakuri"Duka suka Sa Mata Dariya Kafin su Bata hakuri"Ba jimawa Iyayen suka Fito Aliyu ya ce.

 "Me Akayiwa Mimahna Dazu najiyo kukanta"Tana rugawa wajensa ta ce "Yaya ne sukace Wai Baku Sona "Nan ta Basu Labarin Yanda Akayi Wanda Nasren Ta dinga dariya Tana girgiza Kai ta ce"Kun iya Jan rigima my Belah Muje Mu hada dinner Ta Fada Tana Kama Hannun Belah Mimah ta kwala Kuka ta ce.

"Dama Yaya ya ce kinfi sonta Ni bakya Sona Ko hakuri Baki ban ba"Nasren na Dariya ta ce "Mimah rigima Kafin Ta durkusa Tana Hug Dinta ta ce "Rabu Dasu gobe ke da Aunty kawai zamu je wajen Kaka Mai Baku Goruba,Da sauransu ai kina so ko"cikin sauri ta Yi tsalle Tana murna Nasren ta ce "to yi shiru karki Fada Musu kafin su kashe suna Dariya".

Washegari Karfe Ukku a Hadadden Gidan Mama Laure Da Abba ya Bata suka yada zango daga ita sai Mimah Belah Bayan sun jibge mata uwar sayayya Kamar Yanda suke Mata duk zuwa Banda Sayayyar watan Da ake Aiko Mata wasu Saidai su iske wani Kusan kullum sukazo sai mama Laure Tayi kukan farinciki ,Shiyasa ake cewa da d'a da dukiya ba a Musu mugunta bakasan Mai maka amfani ba Gashi dai Bata Haifa ba Amma Rana tsaka ta Tako ariki.

Sun Jima a gidan Wanda Saida ta hadosu da Kayan gargajiya kala-kala Daman Tuni ta tanadar Musu su Belah sai murna suke Dan suna son Kayan gargajiya Kafin suyi Mata bankwana Nasren ta ce zasu Kara Dawowa Kafin su tafi Tunda ita ciwon kafa ke sakota gaba Daman ba abunda take Yan Aiki KE Mata Komai.

A hanyarsu Ta dawowa Suka kaiwa wata mota karo duka salati sukasa suna rirrike nasren wani irin wawan burki direba yaja nauyayyar ajiyar Zuciya suka sauke Nasren na bude Ido Taga Lokacin driver ya fita Yana bawa Mutumin hakuri Tunda shike baida Gaskiya Shiko sai Bala'i yake hakan yasa Nasren cewa su Belah Tana zuwa ta fita Saida taga Ashraf tsaye dukda yakara girma Da jimawa Basu hadu ba baisa ta kasa Gane sa ba tana zaro Ido ta ce

"Ashraf"kamaar daga sama ya ji saukar muryarta a firgice ya juyo Yana fito da Duka idanuwa Biyu ya ce "Nasren da gaske Kece ko gizau"jiki sanyaye ta ce"Ni ce Ashraf Ashe ana ganinku"ta Fada ta na San basarwa"kamar wani sakarai Haka Ya Karaso Inda take yana niyar Kamo Hannunta' tayi Saurin janye Hannunta' Tana girgiza Masa ta ce.

"meye haka Ashraf nifa matar Aure ce"Gabansa ne ya bada daram ya ce "Yanzu Aure kikayi Kika barni Nasren duk Irin soyayyar da nake Miki Kikemun Daman Zaki iya aurar wani bani ba duk Irin jiranki da nayi nakiyin Aure har Allah ya bayyanamun Ke Ashe haukan banza nake innalillahi wa inna ilaihiraji'un".

"Girgiza Mata Kai yayi Yana kamo Hannunta' ya ce"Na yarda dake 💯 Habibty kinsan Yanda nake sonki da kishinki shiyasama har naji wani Irin karkiji Komai Mijinki ya yarda dake dari bisa dari Goben idan Tazo duk Yanda Kuka tattauna kin Fadamun game da lamarin inshallah Indai suna da bukatar taimako zamu Musu"Cike da Jin Dadi Nasren Ta ce "nagode mijina Abun alfahari na "kaafin su bude shafin sabuwar Soyayya.

Belah ko Fad'a ta dinga yiwa Mimah Akan Meyasa ta fadawa Dady saita zaneta ta Kara Fadar Abu ba a tambayeta ba Saida Tayi kamar zata bugeta kafin ta Fara Kuka Tana cewa"Am sorry aunty Bazan sake ba kiyi hakuri"Belah ta ce"Ni ba ruwana Dake"Kuka ta Kuma Fashewa Tana rike kafarta tana bazata sake ba dakyal ta hakura da sharadin ta sake saita zaneta Kuma gobe ta ba mommy hakuri.

 "Bazan sake ba "Allah yayi Muku Albarka "ta fad'a Tana mikewa Ta Shiga Toilet Dan Tayi Wanka Tana tashi Belah Ta Mike Ta Fara Gyara Zanin ganon Ga mamakina yarinya karama ta iya gyaran gado haka lillinke Komai Tayi Tamaida Wajensa Kafin ta Kalli Mimah tace " fita zanyi Shara".

 ,Tana fita ta Fara sharar d'akin tas Saida ta sharesa kafin ta kwalawa Amir Kira ya hado Mata ruwan mooping Yana Hadowa ya Miko Mata ta na farawa Nasren ta fito jikinta Da ruwa Daure da towel Tana zaro Ido ta ce"yanzu angel A kankanin Lokacin Nan Kika gyara Dakin Nan haka,lallai na shaida Training din momy ba banza ba ,Bani Da Complain da Mijin daughter na Indai Akan tsaftane"rufe fuska Tayi ita ala dole Kunya Tana cewa"Kai mommy nidai Kidaina cewa mijina "Dariya Nasren Tayi ta ce "To naji Allah Miki Albarka"amen momynmu"Kibar mooping d'innan Mai Aiki Ta karasa Banason ki wahalarmun da Kanki"make kaf'ad'a Tayi ta ce "um um momy Ni nakeso na samu ladar ai "Murmushi Nasren Tayi Tana Mata kallon tsantsar Soyayya,duk Cikin yaran Nan Babu Mai Tawafin belah,.

Karfe Biyu Surayya ta kirata akan tazo kofar gidan an hanata Shigowa security din Ta Amsa tayiwa magana yabarta ta shigo Tana zaune a palour taga Shigowa Surayya itama ba laifi Daka kalleta kasan Tana Cikin hutu Ba Shiri Nasren ta Mike rungume juna Sukayi suna murnar Ganin juna Surayya na mamakin nasren CE ta koma haka,Kafin su Shiga hirar yaushe gamo Nan nasren ta Bata Labarin abubuwan da suka Faru tun bayan Rabuwarsu Saida ta boye Mata nabila ba yarta bace Ce Mata kawai Tayi Ba jimawa iyayenta suka bayyana. 

Surayya hada hawaye Ashe Nasren dinma duka ta fisu gata da Komai shiyasa D''an Adam ba Abun wulakantawa bane a sanyaye ta ce Bari nabaki Labarin Abubuwan da suka Faru marassa Dadi bayan tafiyarki".

Tunda Abba ya gano Mama Lami Ta koreku A Ranar ya saketa sosai ta Shiga tashin. Hankali ita da yaranta saboda Daman iyayenta talakawane Kuma sun rasu gidan gadon su ma Kika ganshi saikin tausaya Mata dakyal dai da hakuri da Manya sun Shiga magana Abba ya dawo da ita.

 ,sosai Abba ya Shiga nemanku Keda mamanki haka yaya Ashraf Amma Bai ganki ba ,bayan Shekara Biyu da barinku gidan Abba ya kamu da ciwon blood cancer ,Wanda yasha bakar wahala asibitoci kala-kala ba inda ba a kaisa ba An fitar dashi kasashe Wanda Kusan Duka Kud'in Abba Saida suka Kare a nema Masa lafiya Shekara guda yayi Yana jinya Kafin Allah yayi Masa Rasuwa Rasuwar datayi mugun girgizamu ".

sury ta fad'a kafin ta Dora da cewa"Tunda Abba ya Mutu Komai na gidanmu ya Watse suna Gama Takaba Mama Laure ta ce a raba gado kowa ya Kama Gabansa Dan taga momy itakeda Kananun Yara ita yaranta Manya ne Nan aka raba gado inda ta cinye Gidan da muke Ciki ita Kuma momy mukaci motocinsa Da Saura abubuwansa da ba a rasa ba ,Gidansu momy Muka koma kasancewar iyayenta ba laifi suna da rufin Asiri rayuwarmu taci gaba da tafiya alhmdlh dukda ba yanda mukeso 💯 zamu samu ba 

Na kammala karatuna inda nayi Aure mijina alhamdulillahi yana da Hali sosai dan Kasuwa ne yanxu haka yaranmu biyu,momy ma Tayi Aure tun Kafinma Nayi Aure itama mijinta ba abunda Bai Mata Dan Shi yake ma rik'e su khalifa ,bangaren su mama ko kinsan Akace inba Gaskiya Komai naka baya Tafiya daidai,a Hankali,a Hankali suka dinga Saida gadonsu suna cin Abinci Gashi Yaya Ashraf Duk yanda aka buga yaki samun Aiki Daman Abbane yake Dan koyamai business dinsa To Tunda ya Mutu bama jarin da zai Kama.Kasuwar kinsan Kuma Sarai Mutane Rai sukewa da zarar da baka da Rai wani kallo ma baka isheshi ba bare har asa ran abokan Abba zasuyi wani Abu

 saida takai Hatta gidan Da suke Ciki sun sayar Zuly Tayi Aure haka Maimuna Sauran Shidar har Yaya Ashraf na bakwai duka suna Nan Abun tausayi a gidan gadon Mamansu suka koma Idan Kika gansu Baki taba cewa sune saboda Irin yanda suka koma Ga mama Lami ba iya sana'a Tayi ba ta Saba Komai Abba yayi Mata Tana gadara Tana takama Tana take uban kowa yanda yaso sai Gashi yanzu Irin abubuwan da ta dinga yiwa Mutane Tana wulakantasu Shi ake Mata Dan har barar Abinci take Zuwa gidan masu kudi dukda Yaya Ashraf Yana iya bakin kokarinsa na ya tallafa musu kinsan ance Dambu yayi yawa bayajin Mai ,

yanzu Haka wani shago ne yake tsarewa uban gidan sa a kwari motarma Da kikagani Ba tasa bace ta uban gidan ce Saidai Kuma duk wahalar Nan da sukasha Yaya Ashraf Bai Daina sonki da nemanki ba anso yayi Aure dukda baida Jinjinar sana'a Amma yakiya ya ce"KE yake jira"a takaice dai Kinga Irin abubuwan da suka Faru wannan Abun kanshi ya ishi bawa Darasi Nasren wlh Nayi sanyi da duniya yanda Baki tunani,duniya ta na matukar bani tsoro Ashe Da gaskiyar Yan magana da sukace duniya Budurwar Wawa duk yanda ka Dauketa a haka take Zo ma Yanzu ji ba wulakancin da su mama Laure Basu dinga muku ba Karshe Gashi Yanzu ku kuke Cikin Jin dad'in Su kuma sun afka wahala ,Allahsa mugama Da DUNIYA LAFIYA kawai".

"Jiki sanyaye Nasren ta ce"Surayya Ina son Na taimaka Musu,wlh na tabbata bazan iya bacci ba yau muddin Banga Halin da suke Ciki ba Ki taimaka ki rakani gidan please"Numfashi Sury ta ja ta CE "tabbas duk Wani mai'imani dole ya tausaya Musu dukda su sukaja Amma ance maida cuta ga macuci asara ka mayar da alkhairi yafi Idan kin shirya yanzunma muna iya Tafiya".

ta fad'a Tana yafa gyalenta kafin ta kalli su nabila ta Ce"Belah Zanje na dawo Kar ki Bari suyi barna Dan Allah kafin ta kalli Arif ta ce"Banda fad'a Idan kuku ya Gama girki Belah kiyi serving dinku baxan Jima ba"to momy adawo lafiya"Suka fad'a a motar Nasren suka tafi sury na Mata kwatance har suka Isa kazamar unguwar ita Kanta Nasren ta girgiza da ganin Irin kazamin gidan da suke Ciki Dakyal Mama Ta. *JINJINA*
